# 7000-Trinkets

1. 1 complete human skull
2. 15 noxious drow cigarettes in a little tin coffin.
3. 1d100 feet of high-strength fishing line.
4. 3-year old trail rations that look disgusting but smell delicious.
5. 3' golden rope
6. 30' of golden-colored curtain cord. Purely ornamental, can't support weight like a rope.
7. 5 oz bottle of common perfume of a non-exotic scent. Is generic enough to be appropriate for both genders.
8. 6 chicken eggs nestled among straw in a wooden box.
9. A magical acorn
10. A 1 inch wide strip of paper. It seems to always be possible to unfold it again lengthwise, doubling its length, but never making it wider.
11. A 1-centimeter long perfectly functional crossbow.
12. A 1-inch tall lead figure of yourself. Magically transforms to look like a small figure resembling the owner
13. A 1-ounce block made from an unknown material.
14. A 1-pound egg with a bright red shell.
15. A 3 minute sand timer that slows as you focus on it
16. A 3-foot cube box that causes bread placed in it for more than one minute to become toasted and buttered on one side.
17. A 300-page rulebook for rock-paper-scissors.
18. A 5-inch length of enchanted string. It tries to constrict the most nearby living person, acting somewhat like a snake.
19. A 6 inch long crystal coffin. When empty if touched to a dead body the body is shrunken and placed inside the coffin. The body can be released from the coffin by opening it. While in the coffin the body is under the effects of a gentle repose spell.
20. A 6' piece of metal pipe with protrusions that kind of looks like a key
21. A 6 piece of darkmantle leather
22. A 6 piece of dolphin leather
23. A 6 piece of pipe with protrusions like a key
24. A a multitool with only one tool in it. That tool being a magnifying glass that has the words try to find the other tools inscribed on it in Common. The magnifying glass itself gives the one using it more insight into what they are looking at through it.
25. A a violin you found clutched in the hand of a dead old man whenever you play it regardless of the tune it makes people incredibly sad.
26. A amulet filled with liquid that churns, freezes, or boils to match its wearer's mood.
27. A ash elemental in a bottle, made from the ashes of your dead grandfather.
28. A backpack that makes eating sounds when items are put inside.
29. A backscratcher, downsized for use by gnomes.
30. A bag containing forty-seven humanoid teeth, one of which is rotten.
31. A bag containing three history books so out of date they're not even written in modern Common.
32. A bag filled with the smell of pastries.
33. A bag full of tiny mummified frogs
34. A bag of odd mushrooms.
35. A bag of one-hundred marbles, ninety-nine are white and the other is black
36. A bag of psychedelic mushrooms
37. A bag of salt. You have tasted a small bit of it, and amazingly, you can taste the magic in the salt, though it doesn't seem holy.
38. A bag of weird living figurines of everyone you know, including yourself, and they seem to be unable to see you despite your ability to interact with them.
39. A bag that is full of rainbow-coloured sand.
40. A baked clay figurine of a wide-eyed kobold with a bone in its mouth.
41. A ball of clay that grows a new eye every 1d4 hours. 1d4 hours later, that eye disappears. No matter what, the eyes are always staring into yours.
42. A band of iron that shines brilliantly despite being completely rusted.
43. A bandana that makes the wearer look 10 pounds lighter.
44. A bandoleer for crossbow bolts.
45. A bar of soap that smells like rotten meat.
46. A bar of titanium that feels squishy and soft to touch.
47. A beautiful sundress made by a blind seamstress
48. A beautifully crafted doll that belonged to a knight named Beirand.
49. A bedroll that seems to change it's coloration slightly to match the environment
50. A beige jar of red ointment without a label.
51. A bejeweled statuette of a knight that is a replica of a famous sculpture.
52. A bell that awakens anyone within 30 feet when rung.
53. A bell that summons a fox from the nearest unoccupied space. It does nothing but stare at the person who rang the bell for exactly three minutes and forty-two seconds, after which it runs off.
54. A belt buckle stolen from a noble or a king.
55. A belt buckle.
56. A belt made from the preserved tentacle of a Displacer Beast
57. A belt pouch filled with dried catnip.
58. A belt which always fits

63. A bit of folded cloth that, when unfolded, turns into a stylish cap.
64. A bit of rock from a headstone.
65. A bit of slime in a jar. When the jar is opened, the slime tries its hardest to stay as far into the jar as it can, and something tells you you shouldn't touch it.
66. A bit of this barrel’s key is worn as if by much use. The lock it fits is similarly ancient.

411. A dung beetle the size of a pony that refuses to do anything but follow you, and push a gigantic wad of dung.
412. A dwarven beard ring
413. A dwarven scone. Resembles granite, less palatable
414. A fabled lockpick that supposedly never breaks. It seems to have lost its power, however, as it had broken on the first use.

416. A faceless doll made of driftwood
417. A facsimile of a human skull made from interwoven oak twigs
418. A faded writ of trade from Yartar
419. A failed adventurer's suicide note. I should have died down there with the others…

422. A fancy man's deck cleats
423. A fashionable domino mask
424. A feather with a piece of red string tied on the end of the shaft.
425. A fetish made of bone and feathers that lets you cast the ritual Speak With Animals in 1 minute if you can normally cast it as a ritual.
426. A few feet of rope made from spider silk
427. A figurine of a fiend, so lifelike it seems like it might come to life and attack any second.

429. A finely crafted beard plate to armor a beard or worn as jewelry

431. A finely preserved sheep's bladder

433. A fire drake scale that is always warm to the touch
434. A fired bullet made of a silvery, unidentified metal.
435. A fish mask that lets you hold your breath twice as long as normal.
436. A fishing hook that wriggles like a worm negating the need for bait.
437. A fist sized metallic pyramid, deeply etched in incomprehensible runes
438. A fist sized rock that beats like a heart
439. A fist sized turtle shell.
440. A fist-sized ball of wax
441. A fist-sized cog, covered in barnacles
442. A fist-sized green seed covered in brown spines.
443. A fist-sized lump of moss that seems to be breathing
444. A fist-sized metal frog or toad (your choice).
445. A fist-sized sphere; half of which is blue while the other half is red.
446. A fist-sized spool of fine silvery wire, thinner and stronger than anything else
447. A fist-sized spool of fine silvery wire, thinner and stronger than anything you've encountered before. You get the feeling that it would be more likely to cut you than to break if you put your weight on it.
448. A fist-sized stone sphere that rolls after you when you put it down and walk away.
449. A fist-sized stone that glows slightly and feels incredibly hot to the touch.
450. A flag for a country which no longer exists
451. A flag from a ship lost at sea.
452. A flask of blue liquid which smells faintly of urine
453. A flask of giant's blood.
454. A flask that freezes any liquids stored in it at midnight, and unfreezes them at noon.
455. A flask which has had its inside surface blackened by soot
456. A flask with water containing gold flakes
457. A flat disc of layered metal and prismatic glass with a hole in the centre.
458. A flat iron rod with a button on one end. When the button is pressed the rod becomes magically fixed in place until the button is pressed again. The rod can hold up to 80 lbs of weight. A DC 8 strength check allows the rod to be moved up to 10 feet.
459. A flat stone with white chalk marks that change each time it is observed
460. A flat, curved piece of wood that when thrown, either returns to your location or lands on the closest roof if it doesn't hit anything.
461. A flint and tinder that causes any fires light with it to be purple in color
462. A flip book that depicts a cartoonish spine devil operating a riverboat.
463. A floating glass orb that follows you around and makes whirring sounds.
464. A Flower, in a glass vial that heats up when a certain word is spoken
465. A flute in the shape of a skull made out of dead wood that whenever played makes the listeners feel scared.
466. A flute that, when played, makes the sound of a random instrument, though never a flute.
467. A folded paper frog. When unfolded, you can read an uplifting message.
468. A fossil of an extinct many-limbed critter.
469. A four sided die that only rolls 2s
470. A four-leaf clover pressed inside a book discussing manners and etiquette.
471. A freezing cold gauntlet.
472. A freshly-excavated humanoid skeleton the size of your hand
473. A frog statuette from which the legs have been broken off
474. A frost-covered silver locket that's frozen shut
475. A fully articulate plate gauntlet made out of unbreakable, unmelting chocolate.
476. A fur-lined cap that looks like a pile of leaves
477. A furry, feathery pouch made from the hide of a owlbear
478. A game board and pieces for a game no one knows how to play
479. A gear that refuses to mesh with any other.
480. A gem that adheres to your forehead like a third eye. If you spend a Ki point your speed increases by 5 feet for that round.
481. A gem that can summon a dim orb of light that does nothing but follow the summoner for a while.
482. A gemstone that looks like a lump of coal when examined by anyone but you.
483. A gemstone with a bloodshot and constantly roving beholder eye visible deep inside it. Once per day the owner can gain 1d4 to a saving throw against a spell.
484. A genie's veil
485. A genie’s golden arm bracelets.
486. A giant cupcake that has been half eaten.
487. A gigantic blade when used in combat of any kind it becomes immobile and unable to move at all otherwise it's as light as a feather.
488. A gigantic snake's tooth that has an unsettling aura about it.
489. A gilded egg, about the size of your fist. If you listen carefully something can be heard scratching and muttering to itself inside it.
490. A glass bead, hardened by psionics
491. A glass bone
492. A glass bottle of water containing a live Quipper that is too large for the mouth of the container
493. A glass bottle that shines like gold.
494. A glass bottle that spins and points to the same place when left alone
495. A glass bottle with a tiny ship of unfamiliar design inside
496. A glass cup that can only be filled half way no matter how much liquid is poured into it.
497. A glass elf ear
498. A glass eye that spins to look at things other people are looking at.
499. A glass eye with a serial number reading D-1071.
500. A glass eye. Horizontal hourglass pupil like a goat
501. A glass eye. Hourglass pupil like a goat.
502. A glass eyeball that looks about of its own accordance
503. A glass eyeball that looks about of its own accordance, and can roll around.
504. A glass figurine in the shape of a lobster.
505. A glass file; intended for the care of nails, claws or talons.
506. A glass globe of swirling green goop, no openings.
507. A glass jar containing a harmless clear gel
508. A glass jar containing a live goldfish
509. A glass jar containing a weird bit of flesh floating in pickling fluid.
510. A glass jar containing lard with a label that reads, “Griffon Grease”.
511. A glass jar containing the preserved corpse of an unfamiliar aquatic creature
512. A glass jar that appears to contain boiled cabbage, vinegar and several spices
513. A glass jar that has about 12 living, miniature frogs inside of it.
514. A glass jar with tentacles in a pickling fluid
515. A glass lamp with a tiny star suspended inside
516. A glass lens set in a cheap metal rim. The glass is scuffed, but it makes reading slightly easier on weak eyes
517. A glass orb filled with a tiny ooze. It has a sprite skull inside of it.
518. A glass orb filled with moving smoke.
519. A glass orb filled with smoke. If you concentrate on it an image of the person you are thinking about appears.
520. A glass orb filled with water, in which swims a clockwork goldfish.
521. A glass orb that replicates yesterday's weather inside itself.
522. A glass pendant with a hole in the center that a mild breeze always blows out of
523. A glass sphere containing ooze, still alive; it sometimes causes green acidic liquid to gush out of the sphere.
524. A glass sphere with a piece of the ice inside it, on a chain
525. A glass sphere with a piece of the Icewall inside it, on a chain
526. A glass vial filled with nail clippings.
527. A glove worn by someone you deeply admire.
528. A glowing blue-green line, six inches long, but with no discernible radius.
529. A glowing rock that was once worshipped as a god.
530. A glowing rod that drives away dampness and moisture in a 10 foot radius.
531. A gnarled walking staff of living wood that can manipulate tiny objects while you hold it
532. A gnome's hair brush.
533. A gnome's traveling journal. All pages are taken up by lewd drawings
534. A goblin-made key that can lock any door, but unlock none.
535. A gold coin minted in an unknown land..
536. A gold coin that always comes up heads when flipped but when inspected shows two tail sides
537. A gold coin that you can summon back to your hand as long as it’s within 20 feet.
538. A gold finger band that has three small glimmering stones set within; two glow with a faint reddish light while the third glows with a pale white luminosity
539. A gold hexagonal prism filled with dead but moving ants
540. A gold nugget that merchants never seem to want to buy. You remember one merchant in particular who mumbled something about a curse after attempting to sell it to him, though you feel no effect.
541. A gold plated wrench
542. A gold ring that glows with strange runes when heated with a flame.
543. A gold ring. Looking through it grants True Sight to 30 feet, but the field of view is far too narrow to be used in combat.
544. A gold rose pendant that smells like a fresh rose.
545. A gold stud earring that whispers completely useless facts into its wearer's ear.
546. A gold-painted rock.
547. A golden amulet in the shape of a bug on a leaf
548. A golden bracelet, engraved with elvish words commemorating the unity of two great elven houses
549. A golden ring forged in the shape of a serpent eating its own tail
550. A golden ring forged in the shape of a serpent eating its own tail. The serpent is so finely detailed it eyes and fangs are visible as are the tiny scales covering its body.
551. A golden ring with an illegible engraving.
552. A golden tooth-cap that causes the wearer to grow a thick beard and/or chest hair.
553. A gourd with dried herbs. Whispers drift through the air when it is shaken.
554. A grease smeared folio of hand-drawn fish pornography
555. A grease smeared folio of hand-drawn pornography
556. A green coat with numerous pockets, each with a separate piece of a moustache trimming kit inside.
557. A green crystalline key, which seems to be unbreakable and always sparkles in the least amount of light.
558. A green leather pouch full of berries that will never rot
559. A green tonic labeled growth. If anything drinks it they grow an inch each second for 1d4 minutes, then shrink 1 foot each second until they return to normal size. Their equipment changes size too but doesn't increase damage.
560. A green wine bottle that can't break.
561. A green, metal orb that slowly orbits any obese humanoid it's thrown at.
562. A greenish petrified bit of wood that resembles a caterpillar with tiny spots of ruby inlaid along the body
563. A grime encrusted bone ring
564. A grotesque statuette of a humanoid with rat-like features.
565. A group of small glassy spheres, all attached to each other in such a way that they form a rough pyramidal shape.
566. A guide to changing bowstrings full of grammatical errors.
567. A guide to living with a house full of talking objects.
568. A gyroscope with a diamond set in the center and rubies on each end of the main axel
569. A hag's hairpin.
570. A half-brick of cheese in a sock.
571. A half-built sled.
572. A halfling sized set of breeches
573. A halfling war fetish with dried blood on it
574. A halfling's traveling journal. Most pages unused, what little is written makes fun of people's hats and noses
575. A halter that when placed on a cow, donkey, mule, horse, or camel gives you advantage on animal handling checks for that beast.
576. A ham and butter sandwich with lettuce, but the bread is made of cotton.
577. A hammer from a long gone blacksmith. Sometimes at night you can see the hammer float and pound any weapons nearby.
578. A hammer that whispers to you seductively in your sleep and takes pleasure in being used. You are usually creeped out by it.
579. A hand mirror backed with a bronze depiction of a medusa.
580. A hand mirror that holds the attention of anyone looking into it. You feel absolutely fabulous.
581. A hand mirror that reflects an unknown eye at all times
582. A hand mirror that shows everyone's reflection except your own
583. A hand mirror that shows the scene past the mirror as if it had no glass and no back
584. A hand puppet that very much resembles another party member
585. A hand sized bell with no clapper but it still rings when played
586. A hand-broom enchanted to constantly sweep up whatever room it's placed in
587. A handbook of etiquette for nobles of an empire that fell.
588. A handbook that details all the different creatures from another world though you have never seen nor heard of any of them before or has anyone else.
589. A handheld cauldron, that always is a little hot.
590. A handkerchief of waterproof fabric
591. A hangman’s noose that feels heavier than it should.
592. A hard black leather case containing an excellently crafted pairing knife
593. A hard black leather case containing an excellently crafted set of well-used professional kitchen knives, one nearly as long as your arm.
594. A hardback blue book containing a list of every monster in the world and their exact demographics. The book seems very old.
595. A hardened bone slave's collar
596. A hardened sand painting depicting a gladiator match
597. A harlequin mask that makes you feel oddly sad when you wear it.
598. A harmless stage dagger with retracting blade and blood-compartment.
599. A harp which strings play notes you can only hear after sunset
600. A hat that belongs to a violent marauder lord.
601. A hat that drenches the wearer in a viscous orange fluid.
602. A hat that has a secret space on the inside which is the size of a small pouch and very hard to locate.
603. A hat that keeps you magically dry in any rain.
604. A hat that never gets wet.
605. A headless chicken
606. A headless chicken fetish
607. A heart shaped locket that allows you to mentally send up to thirteen words once per day to the person whose name is engraved in the locket. They can immediately respond back with thirteen words.
608. A heavy iron key bearing the name of a ship long lost to the sea
609. A heavy metal coin that floats on water
610. A heavy rust-covered metal rod that whispers words only you hear
611. A hefty piece of magnetic ore that fell from the skies
612. A helmet forged to fit the head of a child.
613. A hemp necklace that amplifies the wearer's voice slightly
614. A hempen anklet that cannot be untied once attached.
615. A hempen anklet that does not need to be tied
616. A hessian sack full of bird feathers in the most fantastic colours imaginable
617. A highly-polished, palm-sized steel orb that always rolls downhill.
618. A holy book devoted to a god you've never heard of.
619. A holy book devoted to the book itself
620. A holy symbol of Amaunator that makes you immune to the exhaustion effects from hot weather.
621. A holy symbol of an elemental priest of water
622. A holy symbol of Chauntea that you can activate once per day to weed, water, and care for a field of crops no larger than half an acre.
623. A holy symbol of Eilistraee that allows you to sing beautifully.
624. A holy symbol of Grumbar that allows you to speak Terran.
625. A holy symbol of Helm that you can activate once per day to gain 1d4 to an initiative roll.
626. A holy symbol of Moradin that makes you immune to the poisoning effects of alcohol.
627. A holy symbol of Myrkul once per day you can use it to animate a tiny beast. The tiny skeleton or zombie remains under your control until it is destroyed or you animate a new undead. (AC 10, 1hp and unable to deal damage)
628. A holy symbol of Waukeen that gives you 1d4 on checks to run a business.
629. A holy symbol of Yeenoghu that once per day you can activate as a bonus action to allow a gnoll within 30 feet to make an attack as a reaction.
630. A horn from a Devil or Demon that allows you to speak Infernal or Abyssal respectively.
631. A horn that has been cut cleanly in half.
632. A horn that when blown can clearly be heard by everyone within 300 feet. After blowing the horn the users’ words are amplified for the next minute and can also be heard by everyone within 300 feet.
633. A horn that when blown through produces no sound

635. A horseshoe once worn by a unicorn.
636. A horseshoe that never becomes worn or needs replacing

639. A hunting horn that sounds like a trumpet when blown into.

643. A jade pendant carved into the likeness of a dragon that turns into black obsidian when worn in the sunlight

645. A jar containing an alchemical salve that is labeled Apply to soles once per week. However, the salve has solidified into a waxy mass.

647. A jar full of petrified wasps.
648. A jar of gooey salve with small eyeballs in it. It heals magical blindness over the course of ten minutes
649. A jar of honey with a religious symbol painted on the side
650. A jar of ink that seems like perfectly normal ink, but when used, it takes a 12 hours to appear
651. A jar of jam, half eaten. The jar has an inscription bearing the name Mason.

653. A jar of live bees

656. A jester's hat.
657. A jeweled goblet that will never, ever spill its contents.
658. A jeweler's hammer with three heads
659. A jeweler’s hammer with a head on both ends.
660. A journal containing stories of the owner's adventures with a bugbear
661. A journal filled with poetry hand-written in Primordial
662. A journal that details the great adventures of a hero you have never heard of, complete with vivid descriptions of nonsensical creatures and terms, all written in messy handwriting, but with impressive diction.
663. A journal with writing about the mass murder of orphans that lived at a temple, the murderer was never found; the writer seems to want revenge.
664. A journal written by a very racist tiefling.

666. A kami doll that has been painted all black
667. A Key crafted of multi-faceted, magically hardened glass. It reflects light in strange many-hued patterns. Crafted to bypass a magical ward of fiendish resilience and complexity it opens the warded area by casting its shadow on the door in a certain location.
668. A Key crafted of multi-faceted, magically hardened glass. It reflects light in strange many-hued patterns.
669. A key ring full of locks without keys.
670. A key that breaks when it's used on a door. It doesn't open the door.
671. A key that can unlock any single unwarded lock, but cannot be removed from the lock until the mechanism is relocked.
672. A key that is so large—almost two-foot long—surely it is only ceremonial in nature; the lock required to fit this monstrous key would be huge in the extreme. It weighs almost 10 lbs. and could be used as a bludgeoning weapon in a pinch.
673. A key that opens any lock you locked.
674. A key to the family crypt.
675. A key whose barrel is pitted and scarred as if it had been splattered with acid. It is exceedingly delicate because of this damage; if used roughly, if will likely snap off in the lock.
676. A keychain holding the head of a broken key.
677. A kilt worn by someone only ever referred to as ‘The Greatest Adventurer Who Ever Lived.’ Along with an certificate of authenticity…
678. A knife enchanted so that the dull edge appears sharp and vis versa.
679. A knife that belonged to a relative.
680. A knitted yarn wand cozy

683. A lady's coin purse containing two sharp fangs

687. A large brass coin with no markings or images on it
688. A large brass hook that vaguely reminds you of a boating implement. Sharpened to a wicked point at one end and threaded for mounting on the other, the metal is polished to a mirror sheen.
689. A large emerald cut in the shape of a baby pickle
690. A large iron key that holds a clever secret–a smaller key hidden in its stem. It is accessed by unscrewing the larger key’s bow. Typically, the larger key does not fit any lock in the dungeon.
691. A large piece of shell from a dragon's egg
692. A large pitcher of liquid that seems to always change flavor.
693. A large skull of a were-chicken. Who knows how he got like that?
694. A large snow globe that houses a tiny kobold and his family.
695. A large sum of gold coins. Upon closer inspection they are made of bone, without looking so closely you'd never be able to tell they were fake
696. A large tooth with a channel running through. Now dry, it once came from a creature that used venom to paralyze or poison
697. A large transparent gem that, when gripped tightly, whispers in Sylvian
698. A large transparent gem that, when gripped tightly, whispers in Terran.
699. A large, metal wheel of cheese that always smells like the ocean.
700. A large, perfectly red apple. It is carved from wood and painted.

702. A large, yellow, wooden exclamation point on a spring.
703. A laurel of flowers which never wilt or die.

707. A leaf from a treant, it's quite large.

709. A leather amulet with a strange rune on it
710. A leather belt that, when worn, glows with faint blue light.
711. A leather drawstring pouch filled with a dozen perfectly spherical polished wood chips. The chips stick together as though magnetic, but come apart again with almost no effort

715. A leather necklace lined with troll fangs

717. A leather pouch full of rainbow colored sand
718. A leather pouch labeled 'interesting rocks'
719. A leather pouch that contains a single wooden token depicting a crab.
720. A leather pouch that contains seventeen sewing needles.
721. A leather pouch that keeps herbs fresh for months.
722. A leather shoe made for a dog.
723. A leather-bound book full of pages made of dried leaves
724. A leather-bound diary that when written in will make the ink disappear and answer back before once more disappearing.
725. A leather-cover, bound book of 34 handwritten pages. It contains the descriptions of a naturalist travelling through the Dessarin Valley
726. A legal deed for a house that doesn't exist.
727. A lens that reverses the color of things seen through it, black becomes white, cyan becomes orange, red becomes blue, and so on.
728. A lepidopterist’s box filled dead moths with skull-like patterns on their wings.
729. A letter addressed to you from a king that has been long dead. It was sent recently.
730. A letter from a relative addressing you by a different name

732. A letter of complaint to a toy shop owner.
733. A letter that can barely be read, smudged by tears and withered by seawater, with the legible parts reading Cam…..ere..on..e'll b..hap…med.y….-N…
734. A letter trusted to you by a fallen stranger, bound for a distant land
735. A life-sized statue of a gnome.
736. A lime-green sandal that is sized for a giant.

738. A list of 33 elven names for a particular dragon known to frequent this area.
739. A list of 33 elven racial slurs for orcs

741. A list of people's names, it gets to four people and then the fifth is smudged and crossed through
742. A little black book that records your dreams, and yours alone, when you sleep.
743. A live beehive full of bees. They're not happy.
744. A live tarantula trapped inside a glass sphere
745. A living drawing of a cow grazing.
746. A loaf of bread made into the shape of a longsword that is stale.
747. A loaf of bread you never ended up eating. It hasn’t gotten moldy, somehow.
748. A loaf of herbed bread wrapped in cloth.
749. A lock of giant hair

752. A locket containing a picture of an ogre and a manticore
753. A locket containing a picture of an unrecognizable child.
754. A locket containing a picture of young halfling couple
755. A locket of hair that is rumored to have come from a famed fire genasi.
756. A locket with a strange rune carved inside.
757. A loincloth that's far too long.
758. A long book full of elven advice, most of which amounts to “stay the course.”
759. A long letter of complaint addressed to a teacher you once had written on it.
760. A long list of miscellaneous items. There’s about 700 of them on the list.
761. A long narrow box of stretched and hardened leather, this scroll case is surprisingly rigid. Investigation reveals, the leather is stretched over a framework of thin, but strong, copper wire
762. A long piece of parchment with precisely 1000 odd trinkets written in very tiny script upon it
763. A long scroll case of bleached bone, that was once the leg bone of some tall creature. Stoppered at one end with a bone plug a leather cap fits over the other
764. A long stemmed flower that always points at the sun and never wilts.
765. A long-winded and ultimately misleading report on the growth of peanuts.
766. A longsword whose plain, unadorned and of solid, functional design holds a wickedly sharp edge. A maker’s mark—a hammer set under a soaring raven—adorns the hilt.
767. A longsword with a hilt that is of horn wrapped with worn leather for better grip. It has a simple crossguard inscribed with the sword’s name, “Gutripper.”
768. A longsword with an ornate basket hilt, which is chipped and dented. The blade itself is highly polished and glimmers in the light.
769. A love letter written by a beggar from your hometown. Funny, you don't remember being given this…
770. A love letter written to the orcish warlord Durgak Stone-Fist, addressed from the high-elven princess Aleria Ulathi. You have no idea who either of these people are, but the letter is lovely.
771. A lovingly crafted travel tankard that makes all manners of drink taste of mead.
772. A lovingly crafted wand made from ivory an finely carved sequoia. It clearly held some great power at one point, but the cracked gemstone at the end implies that that magic is lost.
773. A lucky rabbit's foot with a Made in Caerbannog label
774. A lucky rabbit’s foot with a “Made in Caerbannog” label. It grants +1 AC versus tiny creatures.
775. A lump of coal that appears to be burning but sheds no heat and is never consumed
776. A lump of heavy, grey metal that is always warm to the touch
777. A lute made out of dry grass.

779. A mace whose circular head gleams like the sun when caught in bright light.
780. A mace whose haft is engraved with lurid scenes of battle and death. Some of the carvings have been damaged—probably in combat.
781. A mace whose head was forged to depicts a snarling demon’s head. Dried blood covers the demon’s face and one of the demon’s horns has snapped off.
782. A mace with a haft of dull iron worn smooth through countless hours of use. Similarly the head is dented and chipped suggesting it has seen much combat.
783. A mace with a haft of iron and a small square pommel.
784. A mace with a haft of stout oak, the shaped head is worn smooth on one side.
785. A mace with an iron pear-shaped head that glistens as if it were wet. A leather loop is threaded through the weapon’s haft to make it harder to drop.
786. A magic sphere that replays the most horrid-yet-catchy tune you have ever heard at random.
787. A magical signet-ring that leaves a seal in wax that only the person or people you designate can see

1608. A stick of petrified child's candy

1622. A stone smoking pipe that never needs lighting.

2332. Part of a helmet of a draconian that has been cut into the shape of a face

2335. pen with ink vial, pen is carved of a rare wood with intricate carvings
2336. Perfectly accurate compass points north and south with identical ended and indistinguishable needles

2338. Piece of cloak from the tower of high sorcery

2348. Pocket-size magnet. Attracts wood instead of steel
2349. Pocket-sized Orc-to-Common phrase book; it's not very long at all
2350. Porcelain doll’s head that always seems to be looking at you.
2351. Portrait of a naked elven maiden with a tattoo of a couatl curving down her side

2354. Prayer idol that looks like it is from a prehistoric time
2355. Precentury skull
2356. Protective eyewear. It’s a bit small for a human, but can fit perfectly on any other race, oddly enough.
2357. Pumice piece that is always moist

2359. Ring of Immolation: Sets you on fire.

2362. Ring that makes the wearer appear to be dead and rotten.
2363. Ripped apart piece of a leather cap, For Dad sewn on the inside.
2364. Robe of the Vestalk Clergy

2367. Roughly used paperback novel
2368. Rubber elf ears
2369. Rusty manacles, they emit a woman's scream when opened.

2384. Seven sided die. Looks like a d6 but as your turn it there are 7 distinct numbers

2389. shapes and esoteric glyphs decorating its long, willow shaft. Picked out with pigments of many hues the glyphs and shapes stand in stark relief to the plain white wood
2390. Shed feathers from an aarakocra that have been threaded to make a charm

2401. Silver silk rope. 25'. On the command word, it becomes as hard as steel. A second command word reverts.
2402. Single cheap chrome shuriken. 1d3 damage. Disadvantage vs. metal armor.
2403. Single Dwarf dragonlard candle. Burns with a low red light that won't disrupt darkvision.

2406. Single gold piece with no portrait. Dated seven years in the future

2408. Single nail from the gallows that hung one of the most infamous killer who ever lived.

2410. Single playing card in a suit no one has ever seen

2412. Single scale from a demon's ass.

2421. small book, 100+ pages in length, fictional tale of a girl lost in the wild, author - Sakmir Arlondo
2422. Small bottle of olive oil
2423. Small card marked Get out of jail FREE in Common. King's official stamp or good forgery thereof on back.
2424. Small drums made of psuedodragon skin
2425. Small ear trumpet
2426. Small holes are bored through the mace’s spherical head. When the mace is swung vigourously, the holes create a high-pitched whistling sound.

2429. Soft leather armoring that only covers the right arm, shoulder, and hand. [Provides no AC bonus.]

2431. Solid gold idol of a dretch
2432. Some candy that tastes faintly of pineapple, and never seems to go bad.

2434. Some frog legs made of ivory that bounce around randomly.
2435. Some garish crushed velvet pantaloons, monogrammed with the initials ‘AJW.’ So obviously out of fashion that they can only be antique.
2436. Some random objects such as bones or stones that always come back together when destroyed
2437. Some vials of liquid that create moss on anything they touch. This moss keeps growing and will eventually cover the whole thing. Then the moss withers and flakes off.
2438. Sovereign Goo: The king of all things gelatinous… in a small glass bottle.
2439. spice shaker, thick glass with silver top, contains salt now
2440. Spike of Woodland Suicides. Sort of like a hunter's trap. When left in the woods, wild animals will come and impale themselves on it. A verdant forest yield 1d10 rations worth per day, but must be moved daily. Fey will hate you, though.
2441. Spool of silk from a whisper spider
2442. Staff of the forests: When within 5 feet of a tree or shrub, this staff has a 5%!c(MISSING)hance of animating it. The plant acts according to its nature -- generally seeking out water.
2443. Statue of a hydra with all the heads broken off
2444. Statuette of Baal, 3 tall. A slot in the base can be fed gold which disappears
2445. Statuette of Baal, 3 tall. A slot in the base can be fed gold, growing larger, becoming more complex adding features and companions.
2446. steel tankard engraved with warhammer and mine entrance
2447. Steel washer on a string. When allowed to dangle, it constantly spins while within 100 feet of a construct.

2450. Stone Tablet etched with the True Name of a Demon

2452. Swank leather dice cup.

2454. Talisman of animal teeth that rattle near caves
2455. Tarnished old dog whistle. Can only be heard by mad or rabid dogs
2456. Tattoo needles made from elderfish bone
2457. Tea bags from the Apelands, with the mark of a great Kerchak house
2458. Tea bags stamped with an image of an ape king in a crown
2459. The ace of tears from a card deck. One edge of the card is soaked in blood that never dries.
2460. The ashes of your paternal figure.
2461. The badge of a powerful organisation, with writing etched on the back that defames that group.
2462. The banner of a noble house.

2469. The branch of a tree that caught fire when you were nearby.
2470. The carapace of a breathtakingly beautiful beetle
2471. The carcass of a woodland animal, fashioned into a hat
2472. The combination to a lock, it consists of 5 numbers scrawled on a scrap of paper.

2474. The creepiest old doll anyone has ever seen.
2475. The deed for a parcel of land in a realm unknown to you.

2477. The deed to an invisible hut — at least, that's what the merchant said.
2478. The desiccated body of a small eight-legged black lizard
2479. The diary of a philanderer that contains detailed descriptions of common monsters.
2480. The diary of a prison guard with half of the pages written in a different language.
2481. The dried foot of a quasit on a braided cord for wearing about the neck
2482. The equivalent of two hundred million gold pieces in orange pieces of paper. They go to a game you played when you were little.
2483. The eye of a cat, for some reason it commonly looks around at anything powerful.
2484. The face of an elf, broken off a marble statue.
2485. The face of an elf, broken off a small marble statue
2486. The fantastical skull of a rare hornless unicorn… or at least that's what the merchant who sold it to you claimed it was. He wouldn't have lied to you now, would he?
2487. The feather of a Solar that is always immaculate. While holding it you can speak Celestial.
2488. The fire-blackened claw of some great beast
2489. The fishhook-sized barb from the stinger of a giant bee
2490. The handle of a sword that has rusted in the ground for a thousand years.
2491. The head of a mummy.
2492. The head of a pickaxe that was used in a lost gold mine, with names carved in Dwarvish runes along the sides.

2494. The hilt from a great hero's broken sword.

2498. The hilt of an ancient sword bearing the elvish words: Dawn rises before death's fall.

2500. The holy book of a dead god.
2501. The Ivory Truffle Pig: This ivory statuette of a pig can, once per day, be commanded to grow to full size and seek the nearest source of food, fresh water, or truffles. It remains active for 2 hours before reverting to a figurine.
2502. The journal of a philosopher, full of wise sayings and anecdotes.
2503. The last baby tooth of a young giant, who lost it long ago.
2504. The last page of an unknown diary containing only the words close the door written in what appears to be blood
2505. The left glove of a well-known murderer. You periodically find yourself wearing it.
2506. The left shoulder piece from a set of dwarven plate mail, deeply scarred
2507. The leg of a chair that broke off with a friend of yours sitting in it. You remember using it as a fake weapon when you were young.
2508. The magically preserved eye of an extremely rare and fantastical creature
2509. The necklace of a sibling who died on the day you were born.
2510. The pickled tentacle of an illithid
2511. The playbook from a performance where many of the audience died in a suspicious fire
2512. The preserved finger of a giant that you once knew.
2513. The preserved hand of a famous noble that moves when pointed at the ground.
2514. The preserved skull of a corvid
2515. The right half of a broken bronze circlet with a light leafy pattern that when placed on your head, stays in place as though the other half was still there.
2516. The rowel (the little wheel part) from a spur
2517. The scabbard of a curved dagger, inlaid with patterns of the moon and stars
2518. The script for a never-published play about an author going mad, the writing gets noticeably less neat and more frantic, eventually stopped before the final page
2519. The skeleton of a small bird with hands where its wings should be
2520. The skin of a doll, missing its stuffing and eyes
2521. The skull of a goblin. An intricate pattern of lines and circles has been carved into the top.
2522. The skull of a wolf, peculiar for the rack of antlers sprouting from the top of its head.
2523. The soul of a hero long gone. It does nothing except look pretty and follow you incessantly.
2524. The stuffed and preserved head of a gnoll
2525. The symbol of a powerful religion, covered in soot that stays no matter how much it is cleaned.
2526. The tiny skull of a rabbit that whispers scathing insults when nobody is looking.
2527. The upper portion of the skull of a creature. It appears to be threaded as if it was once screwed into place.
2528. The wooden hand of a notorious pirate.

2533. Three edible insects that induce hallucinations when consumed.
2534. Three green diamonds made of cloth that attach to each other when put near each other but separate when placed near water.
2535. Three links of a silver chain. Each link is engraved with tiny symbols or unknown origin.
2536. Three pairs of shoes that each wouldn't even fit a baby.
2537. Three primitive, flint arrowheads
2538. Three short strands of lead wire braided together
2539. Three silver balls that float about your head.
2540. Three six-sided dice, carved from stone and polished

2542. Three thousand tiny hats that you almost can't see. You can only tell they're hats because you get a wizard to enlarge one for a short period of time.
2543. Three triangular platinum coins bearing the marks of an unknown kingdom

2547. Tiny model ship built inside an empty potion bottle

2552. Tooth from a Unicorn that has been drilled and threaded onto a bright braided string

2560. Twenty-sided dice (Glass, Roman-style).
2561. Twenty-two very small silver tokens, each with a happy face painted on them
2562. Twisted bit of branch almost like a wand. When used to draw on the ground the scratches slowly fill with ink
2563. Two bars of musical notes, which, when played, sound awful.

2588. Vial of unicorn's milk.

2601. Wooden Spoon used in Gully Dwarf cooking, it's large enough to be used as a club

2615. A a squat, round bottle with the bottom half wrapped in twine containing a rare distilled spirit from desert regions known as Aleaqrab. More commonly referred to as Scorpion Whiskey, the liquid is a dark, rusty brown, and is similar in viscosity to maple syrup, though it’s not nearly as sticky. The thickener is hidden at the bottom of the bottle: a scorpion tail, severed from a living creature. The tail is removed and immediately dropped into a full bottle of barley spirit; the mixture of blood and venom give the drink its trademark color and flavor, along with an unusual extra kick. Aleaqrab is traditionally drunk as a shot. It smells metallic and vaguely briny. It has a strong copper flavor with notes of honey, and a piquant burn closer to a hot pepper than regular alcohol. Drinkers of weak weak constitutions often find that the scorpion venom causes their mouth and tongue to go numb for hours after taking even a single shot.
2616. A aged wooden pipe marked with Elminster’s sigil
2617. A alchemist’s rugged leather vest with a multitude of pockets, loops and hooks for holding various items.
2618. A amateur’s woodcarving kit containing simple but serviceable tools
2619. A baby’s blanket made of the finest cloth. It was kept in a mothballed container for years. It smells faintly of mint, as if someone meant to store it for a very long time.
2620. A backpack filled with old scraps of cloth. If examined, they may be placed together to form a map showing the location of an ancient hobgoblin tomb.
2621. A backpack that makes eating, chewing and swallowing sounds when items are put inside.
2622. A badge from a forbidden order of fallen paladins.
2623. A bag containing forty-seven humanoid teeth, one of which is rotten
2624. A bag containing sixteen small leather balls
2625. A bag of charcoal made from a church’s burnt rafters.
2626. A bag of glass eye marbles fashioned in various shades of blue, green, brown, and hazel. When a marble is rolled on the ground, it always appears to be looking at the creature who rolled it.
2627. A bag of milky white marbles with long swirls of pale pinks, reds, blues, greens, and purples decorating them. Though not made from real pearls, they reflect light in a vaguely enchanting manner.
2628. A bag of one-hundred glass marbles; ninety-nine are white and the other is black
2629. A bag of small green scales that sometimes seem to mold themselves into a tail
2630. A bag of very large hazelnuts.
2631. A bag that contains six dice, each with a different number of sides
2632. A baleful gem that glows a sickly green and tingles unpleasantly warm when touched. The sparkling object is less like a precious stone and more like the withering glare of corruption, made corporeal and pellucid in crystal.
2633. A ball of high quality waxed twine with a platinum netting needle stuck through it.
2634. A bandolier from which hang a half dozen small securely stoppered flasks. Each is filled with a noxious substance, preserved at the height of its foulness: Human diarrhea, spoiled milk, vomit, cat urine, skunk stink glands and rotting fish. The flasks are flimsy and designed to break apart when they hit something solid and each stopper has a small eye-hook screwed into the cork. They can be thrown, shot from a sling or flask launcher (A modified light crossbow) or a length of twine has be tied to the eye-hook, creating a tripwire trap.
2635. A banner in black with the image of a crow sewn into it with white silk, surrounded by arcane runes stitched in black thread. Three white silk ribbons flutter from it.
2636. A bar of citrus scented soap that bubbles when used
2637. A bar of lavender colored soap that when used, makes things dirtier instead of cleaner.
2638. A bar of rough soap made from ground pumice, and a little bit goes a long way. Light gray in color, it scrubs off trail dust, sweat, and blood with equal ease, leaving behind a clean, slightly dusty scent.
2639. A bar of steel, that’s been bent into a triangle shape and held by a loop wire at the top curve. Knowledgeable PC’s will recognize this as a musical instrument which is aptly named the triangle
2640. A barber’s kit in a black leather case, consisting of a pair of scissors, a straight razor and strap, a bowl, and a bar of soap.
2641. A barely legible prayer written on leather, dotted with stains. It reads “May vengeance steady your hand with righteous anger. In this den of thieves, murderers, and monsters, there is but one answer, one god, and her name is written in blood.”
2642. A barnacle-encrusted piece of ancient stonework. Its touch fills the bearer’s ears with a great pressure that pulses like a dreadful giant’s heart.
2643. A basalt idol depicted a creature that appears to be half-man and half-fish.
2644. A basilisk egg, tightly bound within a leather pouch and swaddled in a bundle of furs.
2645. A basket of blood fruit, a product of nature magic tainted by chaos and evil. The fruits resemble wan, black, malformed apples and are tautly filled with a mixture of blood and oily, dark ichors. Eating a blood fruit wracks the body with terrible stomach spasms and horrendous digestive issues.
2646. A basket-shaped brass censer on a chain that can safely hold a hot coal to burn incense during sacred rituals. The outer plating is covered with etched images and icons of a well-known fire god.
2647. A bassoon with the bell joint carved into the shape of a dragon’s head that shoots smoke rings when played.
2648. A battered and empty scroll case with the emblem of the king’s messenger service stamped on the lid.
2649. A battered cube of dull metal, inscribed with runes and set with small gemstones.
2650. A battered hilt from a great hero’s broken sword.
2651. A battered leather scroll tube containing sketches of several individuals in suggestive poses. Every subject has a profoundly sad or scared look on their face. The sketches, however, were obviously rendered by a skilled artist.
2652. A battered old longcoat that reeks of alcohol and ash.
2653. A battered scroll tube containing a love letter written in an ancient and forgotten tongue that when held causes feeling of homesickness and loneliness.
2654. A battered tin kettle, slightly warm to the touch. Any liquid placed into the kettle will become something almost, but not quite, exactly nothing like tea.
2655. A battle pennant of black cloth with a single golden eye in its center.
2656. A battle pennant of grey cloth with a black short sword partially wrapped in a white death shroud in its center.
2657. A battle standard made from perpetually maggot-ridden, human skin, stitched from many individual fragments. The maggot’s tracks form disgusting demonic faces clearly visible on the standard’s surface.
2658. A battle standard trimmed in small brass bells with clappers made from bones, attached to a six foot spear, impaling an infant’s skeleton. The standard’s insignia is a broken skull on a tidal wave of blood
2659. A bead curtain with dozens of strings made to fit a standard sized door. Anyone paying attention will notice the beads are actually human finger bones.
2660. A bead, about the size of a large man’s thumbnail, carved from brilliant orange opal with sparkling yellow flaws inside. It looks like a stylized flame or sunburst on casual examination.
2661. A bear-hide scrollcase containing a birch bark scroll covered in tree resin for strength on which is written ancient druidic wisdom.
2662. A beaten copper death mask with garnet eyes.
2663. A beaten leather bag containing various pliers, knives, hooks, shears and mind-weakening drugs strapped to its interior. Knowledgeable PC’s will recognize the symbol of an order of demon hunters sewn into the inner lining, and deduce that these were one of their member’s interrogation tools. Kits such as these were incredibly useful for convincing cultists and evildoers of all kinds to divulge their nefarious plots, as the interrogation tools appear intentionally gruesome. More often than not, the very presence of the cruel looking instruments is enough to induce a prisoner to talk. Unfortunately, these tools also inspire some very convincing lies. The use of these tools (Even by virtue of having the victim see them or describing how they will be used and how immensely painful they will be) grants the bearer advantage on intimidate checks made while questioning prisoners, but they impose disadvantage on all sense motive or insight checks made to determine whether the information gleaned is accurate or not.
2664. A beaten scroll case containing a deed granting the bearer the rights to the Whispering Shallows Silver Mine; which produces a good profit per month when fully operational, with a decent site manager… Unfortunately, the mine is currently inhabited by monstrous spiders and the equipment is rotted and needs to be replaced, the back tunnel leads to the troglodyte clans,who work for duergar, who are trying to keep the drow from expanding, on orders from the mind flayers…
2665. A beautiful deck of cards resting in a strong leather pouch with an etching of a joker on the outside. The same etching is on the back of the cards.
2666. A beautiful glass eye with the initials C.S. etched in the back.
2667. A beautiful hand crossbow that is missing its string, making it useless as a weapon. Runes etched into its handle proclaim its owner to be Eilserk Estreval.
2668. A beautiful painting of a late autumn lake in the forest. The longer you look at it, the more mesmerizing it becomes. As you stare, the leaves on the trees seems to jostle in the wind, the lake seems to breathe as water, and my butts seem to drift ever so slightly into the sunset, like a portal into a perfect glimmer of peace. The only curiosity is a sad old man with a gnarled crown sitting upon a small bench, not quite in the foreground though impossible to ignore. He seems to loom over the lake, and is vaguely familiar… He seems to weep, giving the painting a sense of loss and sadness, as if this world so perfect was dying with him.
2669. A beautiful piece of quartz carved in a strange but unclear style. It is perfectly still until a certain tone is played near it whereupon it then begins to vibrate and move, gyrating sinuously. The carving causes the moving rock to resemble a lithe dancer.
2670. A beautiful white and blue china teacup saucer, decorated with a fierce battle between a grizzly bear and a lion, taking place in the ruins of a blood soaked orphanage. There is no context provided for the gorgeous (If gruesome) art, but it does appear that the lion is winning.
2671. A beautiful white and blue china teacup, decorated with a fierce battle between a grizzly bear and a lion, taking place in the ruins of a blood soaked orphanage. There is no context provided for the gorgeous (If gruesome) art, but it does appear that the bear is winning.
2672. A beautiful, hand-crafted, driftwood figurine of a water nymph.
2673. A beautiful, multicolored glass sculpture that seems to take different shapes depending on the angle it is viewed from. From one angle, a mother and child, from another a proud warrior, all in vibrant color and exquisite detail. There are eight distinct scenes visible, one from each cardinal direction.
2674. A beautifully carved wooden prosthetic arm fitted for a small humanoid, etched with tiny runes in ancient High Goblin, a language now all but forgotten along with the proud culture that produced it, who some say were forerunners of goblins and gnomes alike.
2675. A beautifully crafted doll that once belonged to a knight named “Beirand”, according to the name printed on the bottom of one foot.
2676. A beautifully crafted mandolin with nickel frets and brass inlay.
2677. A beautifully crafted silver pocket watch that functions but the hands tick backwards.
2678. A beautifully painted set of castanets made out of sturdy oyster shells.
2679. A beautifully polished obsidian carving shaped into a jaguar skull. Those that touch it feel a deep connection to the animal world and the earth itself.
2680. A beautifully-written madrigal, the first line reading Your blazing mass negates any prudence.” in Dwarvish.
2681. A belt buckle displaying the personal crest of the kingdom’s ruling family.
2682. A belt pouch consisting of a length of bamboo worn horizontally on the belt. The segmented cane has three fastened openings, and each compartment is lined with padded wool to keep the contents from rattling.
2683. A belt pouch containing a handful of small glass fishhooks
2684. A belt pouch containing a handful of tarnished and clipped bronze and copper coins from a variety of different cultures and kingdoms. All are obviously old.
2685. A belt pouch containing three pounds of hardtack separated into half pound sections.
2686. A belt pouch filled with cleaned sloth claws.
2687. A belt pouch filled with cleaned sloth teeth.
2688. A belt pouch holding a small collection of stylized coasters from different taverns
2689. A belt pouch that has a hidden compartment sewn into the lining.
2690. A bewitched letter which appears to be addressed to whoever is currently holding it, describing their features and personality in adoring terms.
2691. A bewitched slip of paper which, if placed on the bark of a tree, reveals in writing the species of that tree.
2692. A big, gaudy silver plated belt buckle with a rot grub encased in glass at the center and “‘CRAWLER” written in cheap gems at bottom.
2693. A bill from a sorcerer listing an exorbitant amount of gold for a spell to cure a terminally ill child.
2694. A bird’s feather made entirely of copper
2695. A bit of folded cloth that, when unfolded, turns into a stylish cap
2696. A bit of malleable, red metal that generates constant, soft static noise
2697. A bizarre structure of pink, worm-like protrusions, each ending in a clear, bright violet crystal. Knowledgeable PC’s can identify the mineral as cobaltian calcite.
2698. A bizarre, intricately painted miniature sculpture, made of a lightweight material; neither wood nor stone. The figurine bears an uncanny resemblance to a member of the party.
2699. A bizarre, metallic lump of iridescent crystals of concentric geometric shapes expanding outward in rough steps. Knowledgeable PC’s can identify the mineral as bismuth.
2700. A bizarrely shaped paintbrush that, when licked, becomes coated with a sparkling clear substance that smells like the sea
2701. A black and featureless rod as wide as a man’s wrist and seems to be made of metal. One end shapes itself to the grip of its holder.
2702. A black and purple scale of some enormous horror of the far realm.
2703. A black bandanna that makes the bearer look ten pounds lighter.
2704. A black basalt statuette of a lion wearing a gold crown and crushing slaves beneath its paws.
2705. A black ceramic theater mask shaped in a caricature of a sad frowning face.
2706. A black chunk of obsidian that is roughly a round shape and has no sharp edges. Looking into its butty depths one can see an almost infinitely receding sea of gold, white, and blue flecks of colour. When held, the bearer will swear that he can hear the sound of perfect silence, the call of the infinite void…
2707. A black coin with two grim sides. One side bears the staring visage of a spiral-horned woman with mean eyes. Its opposing face displays an eyeless skull. A tarry blackbird, ragged, follows the carrier of said coin at cautious distance, watching with fish’s eyes.
2708. A black domino mask that covers the area around the eyes and the space between them. It has small holes on each end, suggesting it could be held on by string or a leather thong. Knowledgeable PC’s will know that traditionally these masks are held on by spirit glue, resin or tree gum rather than strings.
2709. A black executioner’s hood
2710. A black fan made of kobold skin with graphic images of kobold torment.
2711. A black glass bottle that becomes transparent when the full moon shines on it. The moonlight reveals fine etchings that form a pirate treasure map
2712. A black glass orb eight inches in diameter, that appears to have octopus tentacles that reach to grasp the inside of the sphere. Often, they separate, revealing one large cephalopod eye.
2713. A black iron brazier that causes anything burned in it to give of thick, black noxious smelling smoke.
2714. A black iron spur from a devil’s scourge.
2715. A black lace veil which feels oddly cold to the touch. If worn, the bearer immediately becomes aware that it was placed on a corpse during a funeral and stolen soon afterwards. The owner of the veil will be subject to recurring nightmares involving grave robbing and being slaughtered by the disturbed corpses of the dead they’ve looted.
2716. A black leather bag containing a compact set of used torture implements.
2717. A black leather doctor’s bag containing all the accouterments a phrenologist needs to measure skulls. The bag contains several metal skull calipers of various sizes, a labeled chalkware bust of a humanoid head, and numbered charts of skulls of various species.
2718. A black leather dog collar set with white onyx studs.
2719. A black leather domino mask once worn by the Queen of Thieves,
2720. A black leather half-mask that covers the bearer’s nose and mouth and muffles their breathing.
2721. A black linen sleeping mask that covers the bearer’s eyes during sleep to stop light from bothering the bearer.
2722. A black medical bag containing five rolls of bandages, five sealed packets of herbal tea that relieves pain, and a flask of alcohol used for disinfectant.
2723. A black metal box that makes the sound of a whimpering dog when you shake it
2724. A black metallic whistle that is hard to focus on, as it seems to shift about like smoke. When blown, no sound can be heard except that of hounds howling in the far distance.
2725. A black obsidian crystal sphere filled with a dark inky fluid. Inside the sphere is a small white dodecahedron with black text on each of its faces. The sphere gives off a faint divination aura.
2726. A black parasol of delicate design which appears to be mostly decorative. The bearer invisible to those inflicted with lycanthropy when used under a full moon, however it does not mask sound or scent.
2727. A black pirate flag adorned with a dragon’s skull and crossbones
2728. A black pirate flag adorned with a dragon’s skull and crossbones design.
2729. A black robe covered in tattered and worn crow feathers, almost giving the illusion of wings when the arms are raised.
2730. A black rock with blue cracks that’s always vibrating.
2731. A black shiny disk with dozens of embossed rings.
2732. A black silk choker, with a square orange stone set in its center.
2733. A black silk cowl, easily capable of obscuring the bearer’s face if pulled low enough.
2734. A black silk eye patch, adorned with a skull and crossbones worked in silver thread.
2735. A black silk robe embroidered with adamantine thread in an elegant waterfall pattern.
2736. A black silk scarf, lined with raven’s feathers.
2737. A black stone that beats like a heart and seeps red fluid when pricked.
2738. A black triangular battle pennant that gives off a rich purple glow when raised in combat. It is said that the magical light can nullify pain but it does not seem to work for most creatures.
2739. A black velvet bag containing 13 bronze coins of outdated denominations
2740. A black velvet bag containing six beef bullion cubes. When a cube is put into a large pot of boiling water, it becomes a hearty and delicious stew.
2741. A black velvet cloth inlaid with golden thread wrapped around a dried and perfectly preserved red rose. The rose’s thorns are yet sharp and its flower emits a particular heady scent.
2742. A black velvet mask in the shape of a spider with four jointed wire legs protruding from each side of it. It covers the wearer’s face completely but does not hinder vision or speech.
2743. A black velvet mask stitched with silver thread in a spiderweb pattern
2744. A black wig made of the fur of a werewolf.
2745. A black wooden pipe that creates puffs of smoke that look like skulls
2746. A black-green beeswax candle decorated with carvings of birds. The wick seems to be made out of gold threads. It faintly smells of ash and seawater.
2747. A black-lacquered pyx decorated with pornographic images. On the sides and the lid of the small box, colorful hand-painted scenes of lurid degradation depicts men and women copulating not with one another but with jackals, hyenas, goats, and serpents. The box is brimming with coal-black crackers flecked with red. The unleaved bread has a faint but repellent odor or herbs, sulfur and vomit
2748. A blackened diamond corrupted in a failed resurrection attempt.
2749. A blacksmith’s block puzzle made of glass.
2750. A blank book whose pages refuse to hold ink, chalk, graphite, or any other substance or marking
2751. A blank envelope containing a single jigsaw puzzle piece and a note that reads “You have two months to find the rest of this puzzle. If you do not, the entire population in this forsaken area will die.”
2752. A bleached headdress made from the bones of humanoid hands which have been cleverly fashioned together.
2753. A bleached white jawbone once belonging to a dwarf. It shouts insults in dwarven whenever it is touched by an elf.
2754. A blob of clay that takes on various mysterious shapes when left alone
2755. A block of aged-cheese sealed in wax
2756. A block of pink salt, the size of a large brick, with a hollow interior and holes for ventilation that suggests that it can be used as a tea candle lamp.
2757. A blood red fiddle that seems to have strings made of human veins. The music produced by it always sounds horrible and terrifying.
2758. A blood red mask made from carefully sculpted bone, shaped to look like the face of a grinning demon.
2759. A blood red mask made from carefully sculpted bone, shaped to look like the face of a grinning demon. The eyes of the mask are the only parts that are open, with the eyes of the bearer appearing bright crimson while looking through it.
2760. A blood-drained, shrunken ear that appears to be a souvenir taken for subduing the guilty.
2761. A blood-stained set of manacles.
2762. A bloodstained jewellery box containing 2d10 feminine ring fingers, with wedding rings still attached
2763. A bloodstained scrap of parchment with a list of several names, including a couple of the PC’s. All but one of the non-PC names are crossed out.
2764. A bloody cloth handkerchief in which three teeth are wrapped up in. Each has a small gold filling. The teeth are badly cracked and scoured, it looks like someone used pliers to extract them.
2765. A bloody severed hand that grips onto things and doesn’t let go.
2766. A blooming rose carved from red coral.
2767. A blowgun that, when blown through, shoots small, horribly inaccurate pebbles
2768. A blue and gold diviner’s scroll covered in text that change every morning at dawn. The writing is usually cryptic message about future events.
2769. A blue blade shard that hums. If a creature holds the piece for long enough they will start hearing voices that urge him to kill and claim souls for it.
2770. A blue crystal that glows slightly when held
2771. A blue sash cut from perfectly hydrophobic cloth.
2772. A blue silk headband with a single elven rune embroidered in its center.
2773. A blue steel mask that resembles a face at rest, emotionless and cold to the touch when not worn.
2774. A blue, glowing crystal shard the size of a pinkie finger
2775. A blunt carving knife with the word ‘toothpick’ in the language of giants carved into the handle.
2776. A blunt knife with an ivory handle.
2777. A boar tusk scroll case encircled with silver bands.
2778. A board covered with runes and a silver weight tied to a string. Holding the weight over the board causes it to slowly spell the answer to any question asked. The response is never correct (Except by coincidence) and is always just the answer the person asking most wants to hear.
2779. A bobbin of thick string with which it is impossible to tie knots
2780. A boldly colored quartz the size of a pigeon’s egg, etched and painted in such a way that when it is placed to one’s eye in the light, they see a clever but naughty image of a beautiful person in the nude.
2781. A bolt of coarse burlap cloth wrapped in a protective oilskin case.
2782. A bolt of coarse, beige, jute cloth wrapped in a protective oilskin case.
2783. A bolt of fine quality cotton wrapped in oilskin.
2784. A bolt of fine quality grey fleece wrapped in oilskin
2785. A bolt of fine quality grey wool wrapped in oilskin
2786. A bolt of fine quality white linen cloth wrapped in a protective oilskin case.
2787. A bolt of fine quality, blue denim cloth wrapped in a protective oilskin case.
2788. A bolt of green-dyed linen with a total of ten square feet of fabric.
2789. A bolt of ironcloth silk, sewn by the clockspiders of ancient Dolm
2790. A bone and silver corkscrew on a leather thong hung with rabbit’s feet.
2791. A bone case containing black votive candles that burn with a green flame and can only be quenched by blood, not water.
2792. A bone clip inlaid with gold that resembles the teeth of some long dead rat.
2793. A bone ear peg fashioned from the tooth of a desert tiger.
2794. A bone insignia of military rank originating from the legendary Horde of the Endless Dead
2795. A bone key of a design akin to the nomadic desert tribes
2796. A bone pipe carved with intricate crimson sigils; its smoke appears as writhing shades of the damned.
2797. A bone smoking pipe. Its stem is too short for the smoking of poppy-seed and the bowl is too small for any substantial amount of tobacco.
2798. A bone-like object the size of a forearm made from a hardened mixture of animal marrow and minerals. Knowledgeable PCs will know it as a whet bone, which are given to domesticated carnivorous animals to keep their teeth sharp.
2799. A book that tells the story of a legendary hero’s rise and fall, with the last chapter missing
2800. A bottle of a vintage wine that has an odd metallic taste.
2801. A bottle of clear liquid whose flavor changes to match whatever you most desire
2802. A bottle of Lieutenant Morgan’s spiced rum.
2803. A bottle of liquid that releases bubbles that glow in the dark
2804. A bouncy ball made of a strange spongy wood
2805. A bouquet of dead roses that never crumble
2806. A bouquet of flowering purple thistles in full bloom wrapped together in a cone of fresh bramble vines. The plants have been magically enchanted to remain fresh and healthy, they never wilt or fade. The floral arrangement is covered in spikes and thorns and there is no place safe to touch or hold it without somehow being pricked. It’s probably some sort of artistic statement on the pain of beauty or love.
2807. A bowl carved from the bone of an undead creature. The necromantic magic still courses through the bowl causing it to wobble slightly when raw meat is placed in it and causes any foodstuffs placed inside of it to spoil twice as fast as it normally would.
2808. A box compass, hard leather, scuffed, and no larger than a snuffbox. Reveals a sphere struck through by a red needle, suspended in a crystal ball filled with clear liquid. The needle points north, even if rotated in three dimensions.
2809. A box containing a dozen bandages which, when applied to fresh wounds, enhance the size and permanence of scars that will form from them. In addition they function as regular bandages.
2810. A box containing a small pair of gold hoop earrings. A paper inside says “Lidia’s first”.
2811. A box containing an eyeball made of petrified wood that changes color at random every hour
2812. A box made of petrified, grey wood. Square, palm sized, and unusually heavy, it has a lid and iron hinges, but no latch. A face, moon-like and squinting mirthfully, is carved on the immovable lid.
2813. A box made to resemble a heavily pregnant woman with fangs instead of teeth. Her copious belly contains a rather grotesque image of an infant with three heads, each with an open mouth like a key-hole.
2814. A box of peanuts that feel like they are made from metal, but can be eaten as though they were normal.
2815. A box of twenty oddly shaped stones in different shades of red that can be used like chalk but never run out
2816. A box of wooden toothpicks that each have a different and sometimes strange flavor
2817. A box with a tiny group of musicians in it who play when it is opened and look horrified when it is closed
2818. A brace of half a dozen humanoid scalps. All of the scalps bear the same strange tattoo which knowledgeable PC’s will recognize as originating from an evil cult. Perhaps the local city has some sort of bounty for them…
2819. A bracelet consisting of six small feathers tied together with twine.
2820. A bracelet made from a lattice of woven brass. It automatically adjusts itself to the wrist size of its bearer.
2821. A bracelet made of brass triangles each with a face of anguish carved into them.
2822. A bracelet of bone beads carved into skulls, the eyes are polished jet.
2823. A bracelet of halfing design, carved from a single moss agate.
2824. A bracelet resembles a large metal centipede that wraps around the bearer’s wrist. It is made of a dark, glossy metal of unknown origin.
2825. A braided copper bracer that is uncomfortably warm to the touch.
2826. A braided lanyard bearing the words “BEST FRINEDS”. When the wearer of this braided lanyard closes their eyes, they experience the sensations of having warm sun shining on their face and a gentle breeze tousling their hair. The scent of campfire smoke can be smelled, and the laughter of children can be heard.
2827. A brand, or perhaps a rosette iron. Small, and quite delicate in the handle. Scorches a pressed surface with a two-inch-tall capital M if heated cherry-hot.
2828. A branding iron the length of a human hand, whose business end is forged in the shape of the phrase: “Random Motto”. The brand could be used to stamp leather or flesh and might have been used by an individual or organization to mark its goods, armor or members.
2829. A brass and crystal hourglass that when turned over plays softly tinkling chimes as the sand passes through it for the unit of time known as a “song”, lasting a minute or two.
2830. A brass and steel orrery, animated by clockwork and magic in real time. The spheres are nearly perfectly aligned.
2831. A brass bas-relief, depicting the finder’s father, mother, sibling, or childhood imaginary friend, in a gilded cage.
2832. A brass bell that always stays highly polished and resembles the sound of strange laughter when rung. It is rumored to open doors to the Feywild.
2833. A brass bicep bracer with an Efreet motif.
2834. A brass bracelet stolen from a very minor deity, whose holy symbol is etched on the inside of the band.
2835. A brass brazier engraved with holy symbols of a number of evil gods
2836. A brass bust of a famed scholar and medic.
2837. A brass candelabra etched with filigree pattern.
2838. A brass candlestick with a jeweler’s mark stamped into the base.
2839. A brass censer dangling from lead chains that emits smoke resembling writhing vines.
2840. A brass chalice with chilling imagery of demons and tormented humans.
2841. A brass doorknocker shaped like a grumpy old man’s face.
2842. A brass face mask that when worn, insults the bearer’s outfit.
2843. A brass framed bifocal monocle on a silken cord.
2844. A brass hand bell that smells rancid and perpetually covered in a thin layer of grime. Creatures sleeping near it always have pleasant dreams.
2845. A brass handled battle horn made from a conch shell.
2846. A brass lantern that burns pine sap as easily as lamp oil. Burning sap seems to keep small insects at bay.
2847. A brass lever nutcracker with head shaped like a bird of prey.
2848. A brass monocular telescope, etched with decorative markings, but due to poor maintenance, stuck in its collapsed state.
2849. A brass mug with intricate jade inlay
2850. A brass nose flute.
2851. A brass orb etched with strange runes
2852. A brass plated orc skull.
2853. A brass prosthetic nose
2854. A brass ring that never tarnishes
2855. A brass spyglass that always shows the environment suffering a terrible storm
2856. A brass spyglass that triples the range at twice the user can accurately see but always shows a benign, peaceful sight. If there are hostile creatures, they appear as peaceful (goblins appear as deer, or trolls as trees, for example) and dangerous objects or situations appear pleasant and charming.
2857. A brass statue of a winged wolf with quartz fangs.
2858. A brass-colored disc that occasionally bounces up onto its edge and spins several times before falling over again.
2859. A brick made of strange green stone that contains undecipherable hieroglyphs.
2860. A brick taken from a haunted house. The brick grows sharp, jagged teeth at night which retract during the day.
2861. A bright green feather
2862. A bright patchwork satchel containing three juggling balls, three colored handkerchiefs, a deck of playing cards and clownish makeup meant for stage magicians, buskers and other street entertainers.
2863. A bright pink dagger scabbard that feels lighter than it actually is.
2864. A bright red square tablet of unknown material about three inches to a side with a metal plate that slides to open a tiny window through the tablet that reveals a sheet of black material within. It is lighter than stone, metal, or wood and bears no markings other than a rectangle of gummy residue on one side and a small circular metal coin on the reverse.
2865. A bright red, smooth, rounded rod as thick as a human’s wrist. It seems to be made from stone, though it is soft to the touch.
2866. A bright red, strip of cloth, stitched with images of a cheering crowd throwing garlands toward a chariot. It fits across the bearer’s shoulder and then diagonally down their chest to reach their opposite hip.
2867. A bright yellow building brick. Knowledgeable PC’s will determine that due to the wear patterns the brick was used as part of a roadway rather than a building.
2868. A brightly colored maple-wood case filled with an assortment of cheap costume jewelry and makeup. Tucked away in a latched interior compartment is a paper flyer advertising “Gammul’s Traveling Troupe – Magicians! Acrobats! Dancers! Players! See the finest entertainers in seven lands!”
2869. A brightly colored, waterproof leather pouch containing three dozen paper packets filled with various powders. Each packet has a Random Color (Reroll any red and oranges) written on it. When a packet is thrown into a fire the powders ignite and the fire burns with the color written on the paper for ten seconds, after which the flames return to normal.
2870. A brightly coloured starfish that’s been perfectly preserved and encased in a clear glass star.
2871. A brightly polished tin candle holder covered in silver snowflake decorations.
2872. A broad and cumbersome bronze mortar and pestle for mixing alchemical agents. It is inlaid with enameled alchemical symbols.
2873. A broad cavalier hat that’s a bit old, but it’s still fairly stylish.
2874. A broad-brimmed straw hat with a green linen band
2875. A broken compass that only ever points to the nearest other broken compass.
2876. A broken crow skull. The missing pieces of the skull have been replaced with carved, polished and smooth green crystal.
2877. A broken half of a crystal sphere, which hums and shakes just before rain storms.
2878. A broken holy sword, inscribed with the name of a legendary Paladin. Its magical blessing has faded but a skilled smith and enchanter might be able to repair it.
2879. A broken lump of dark gray rock that shimmers like a rainbow along its jagged surfaces. Knowledgeable PC’s can identify the mineral as titanium quartz.
2880. A broken music box which still plays a hauntingly familiar but unrecognizable tune, when wound beneath the light of the full moon.
2881. A broken spear head made of bone. There is an unknown script engraved upon the surface.
2882. A broken sword hilt with strange runes on the remains of the blade.
2883. A broken sword-hilt made of black metal. On it is written “Whoever recovers my blade…”
2884. A bronze and beech incense burner set with several cinnabars.
2885. A bronze ashtray of a sleeping dragon.
2886. A bronze bowl engraved with pagan figures, one side shows a city at war and another shows it at peace. The metal of the bowl is corroded, gone all green and black.
2887. A bronze brooch in the shape of a feather that lets off a faint glow.
2888. A bronze brooch of an maple leaf.
2889. A bronze calligraphy pen covered in filigree patterns. When used as a writing utensil, the bearer will be incapable of stopping themselves from adding an “e”, or that languages equivalent, to the end of every word.
2890. A bronze candelabrum depicting angels being chased by stirges.
