3430. A filthy, mummified monkey’s paw, curled into a fist and clutching what looks like an egg.
3431. A fine ceramic plate
3432. A fine clay pipe, the bowl formed into the shape of a bearded man with a scarred face. The face is so detailed, that the bearer can even make out the arrow shaped earring, which Knowledgeable PC’s will recognize as the mark of a well-known pirate gang.
3433. A fine doublet of incredible intricacy and beauty, with a pattern of nymphs playing in a garden along the back.
3434. A fine hide collar with bone studs.
3435. A fine pine box bound with a leather strap. Inside are five hand rolled cigars. They smell of spice, toasted almonds, and honey. They look quite expensive.
3436. A fine ring case festooned with silver filigree, but with no ring inside. A false bottom reveals a coin sized, obsidian disk, depicting a black raven with three eyes.
3437. A finely beaded women’s handbag. The beads are made of glass, and the different colors have been sewn into an image of a sandwich.
3438. A finely crafted leather backpack that seems to have been handmade from excellent materials by a master’s needle and knife. The style is simple, but durable and effective.
3439. A finely crafted silver hand mirror. Whomever looks into the mirror will see an idealized version of the person they are currently thinking of.
3440. A finely crafted wooden figurine of a blue dragon.
3441. A finely made wool rug six feet in length that depicts a scene of a scene of siege warfare. The battle is portrayed quite realistically with the notable exception of an armed halfling being flung over a city wall by a trebuchet.
3442. A finely tanned, soft leather pouch filled with thirty-six small, polished hematite tiles about 2 cm across, inscribed with non-magical glyphs on both sides. Some of the tiles have different glyphs on opposing sides. The pouch has a leather drawstring.
3443. A finely wrought chain barding covered in a knitted sweater for a dog.
3444. A finger length shard of ice that never melts.
3445. A finger lengthed fish with brightly coloured scintillating scales that’s been perfectly preserved and encased in a rectangular block of clear glass.
3446. A finger lengthed obsidian dolphin figurine which is always wet to the touch.
3447. A finger lengthed stone dog figurine that is always warm to the touch
3448. A finger sized tail made of green glass that occasionally moves.
3449. A finger-sized shard of blue crystal that’s constantly shedding flakes and chips of crystal without ever getting smaller.
3450. A fire opal carved into a small coin. The obverse sigil is a picture of rainfall. The reverse is an elven phrase that translates to “Let us take what nature will not provide.”
3451. A firmly locked, steel chest, marked on the lid in multiple languages that this chest is ordered sealed by order of (Insert setting appropriate authority figure here), opening the chest is a crime, and that punishment for said crime is dismemberment and / or death. If opened, the container is found to be empty with the exception of a hastily scribbled note that reads “I.O.U. one Mcguffin”.
3452. A fish skeleton, tied together with thin wires.
3453. A fishing line that never tangles or snags.
3454. A fist sized ball of an unknown material. When dropped, it doesn’t bounce.
3455. A fist sized ball of copper wire.
3456. A fist sized ball of melted copper coins.
3457. A fist sized bronze cube that, when tapped on thrice, disappears for one minute and then reappears in the same spot
3458. A fist sized chunk of amber with a small (And presumably dead) lizard trapped inside.
3459. A fist sized chunk of good quality bismuth crystal.
3460. A fist sized chunk of meteor rock.
3461. A fist sized chunk of raw silver ore.
3462. A fist sized crystal ball on an ebony stand that radiates divination magic if examined.
3463. A fist sized crystal that looks like it holds trapped smoke. It is easily scratched.
3464. A fist sized cube made of smooth gray stone, which expands when squeezed and reveals a network of deep crimson veins.
3465. A fist sized cube with tiny animals wandering on each face that change when they cross onto a different face.
3466. A fist sized egg which seems to be made of shell and steel in equal parts. It occasionally shifts slightly but you have no idea when it will hatch or what’s inside of it
3467. A fist sized glass orb filled with scintillating colours and pinpoints of light swirling in a nebula.
3468. A fist sized glass orb that drips perfumed water intermittently
3469. A fist sized human skull carved out of jade.
3470. A fist sized iron ball that only rolls uphill.
3471. A fist sized metal sphere that plays quiet, unpleasant, disturbing music when looked at by only one person
3472. A fist sized metallic sphere that sounds a chime every 28 hours
3473. A fist sized obsidian ball that offers a terrible glimpse into the Realm of Chaos, revealing fragments of the future in the swirling vortex of maddening energies.
3474. A fist sized piece of jagged garnet. The deep crimson and maroon gemstone pulses faintly and smells of blood.
3475. A fist sized pyramid made of a heavy metal that always stands straight up from its narrowest end
3476. A fist sized rock that “beats” like a heart.
3477. A fist sized snow globe, with a sturdy base of polished oak, depicting a small farmhouse in the midst of a forest clearing. Shaking the globe causes the snow to gently fall, and causes miniature figures exit the house and begin performing chores about the farmyard. The figures return to the house as the last flake falls.
3478. A fist sized sphere constructed from rings of copper, iron, and silver
3479. A fist sized stone carved to look like a head. It occasionally speaks, asking pointless questions that change each time.
3480. A fist sized stone with a miniature sword stabbed deep into it.
3481. A fist sized, dried, brain coral.
3482. A fist sized, featureless, black perfect sphere that gives off no reflection or shine whatsoever.
3483. A fist sized, polished obsidian orb.
3484. A fist sized, sealed, glass globe of swirling green goop.
3485. A fist sized, terrifying, shapeless mass of horror that fills the mind of the creature who holds it with dread. Those who has seen it cannot properly describe its form afterwards and only remember the fear it filled them with.
3486. A fist-sized brass orb of interlinking whirling cogs.
3487. A fist-sized glass bauble with beautiful coruscating colored lights inside.
3488. A fist-sized glass sphere filled with a black, gelatin like substance.
3489. A fist-sized kidney stone that was passed by a dragon.
3490. A fist-sized lump of mottled grey and brown stone that appears to have been melted by some form of intense heat. Dried “waves” have flowed down the stone’s flanks before hardening. The stone is the only surviving fragment of an elder earth elemental, petrified by a vile enchantment long ago. A fragment of the elemental’s sentience lurks within.
3491. A fist-sized metal frog
3492. A fist-sized orb that resembles the eye of a dragon and dangles from a heavy gold chain.
3493. A fist-sized polished globe of opaque silvery quartz
3494. A fist-sized pyramid of felt-like substance that pulses slowly but steadily.
3495. A fist-sized red stone said to be the fossilized heart of a fire giant.
3496. A five foot square camouflage net made from a loose mesh of twine and varicolored cloth that can be used to disguise the presence of creatures or large objects. This netting is designed for an X terrain and wouldn’t be much use outside of that environment —Note: The DM can choose any appropriate terrain or roll on the following table: 1, Forest. 2, Swamp. 3, Desert. 4, Grasslands. 5, Snow and ice. 6, Mountainous.
3497. A five foot square wire net with clusters of small copper bells hanging at each corner. The net is designed to blanket gear while its owner sleeps, hunts, cooks, or otherwise cannot directly attend to it, and the bells ring out a noticeable alarm if the possessions are disturbed.
3498. A five inch gnome statuette that appears as if it is on the verge of speaking when it is almost out of view.
3499. A five inch metal gear that turns on its own when exposed to sunlight.
3500. A five pound canvas bag of bird feed, containing a mixture of different seeds and dead insects that is suitable nutrition for small birds such as ravens, crows, and chickens. Birds of prey such as falcons, hawks, and owls can also sustain themselves on this, though they typically require at twice as much per day or to be further supplemented with small scraps of meat.
3501. A five pound canvas bag of carnivore feed, consisting of various kinds of meat that have been jerked, smoked and salted for preservation and can serve as adequate travel food for any meat eating mammal.
3502. A five-day pack of various preserved rations, all of which taste like chicken.
3503. A five-sided lantern made of steel. Instead of clear panes, each side of the lantern has a glass stained in a deep red hue. When lit, it bathes the area in an oppressive dark red light.
3504. A five-sided lantern made of steel. Instead of clear panes, each side of the lantern has a random pattern of stained glass. When lit, it bathes the area in bright multi-colored light cast from the lantern’s colored glass.
3505. A flagon that constantly sweats terrible tasting ale.
3506. A flamboyant masquerade mask with large, rare feathers coming from one side. There’s a small chip under the left eye.
3507. A flamboyant, wide-brimmed blue hat with a giant golden eagle’s feather stuck in the band.
3508. A flask made of a metal that resembles a light gold with a curious dark iridescence tinting its lustre. The container is cast with a face on four sides, each bearing a different expression. Their eyes glow with a bloody purple light.
3509. A flask with an unknown liquid. It cannot be poured out unless it is standing upright (In which case nothing happens since gravity). The flask is very sturdy and in inscription reads; “Those that drink smart and slow will drink this drink made long ago.’’
3510. A flat brass clockwork dial so richly studded with circles and hands and curious symbols that it looked like a cross scowly face.
3511. A flat disc of layered metal and prismatic glass with a hole in its centre
3512. A flat, otherwise unremarkable pearlescent-white overcoat button about an inch across. It is heavier than it should have any right to be. It practically vibrates with barely contained magical force.
3513. A flat, round gray stone ring the size of a coin worn smooth by water and time with an attached tag reading “Shieldmeet 1120 DR, is this the key?”
3514. A flathead screwdriver
3515. A fleshy ball the size of a large man’s fist. Dozens of tiny mouths appear, disappear and reappear at strange intervals, each one constantly groaning and muttering unintelligible words.
3516. A flexible metal model of a creature you’ve only ever seen in your dreams
3517. A flexible skin tight, black-silk mask that covers the bearer’s face with just a slit exposing the eyes and perforations at the nose and mouth.
3518. A flimsy pine box that contains six teabags containing unknown herbal mixtures. Drinking the tea produces a random physical effect based on their body’s reaction to the mixture. None of the bags are exactly alike and each batch of tea produces its own random result. —Note: The DM can make up their own reaction, roll a D6 or choose from the following effects: 1, The drinker hallucinates vividly and believes they are fighting a dragon for the next five minutes, but they are actually in a harmless comatose state. 2, The drinker becomes groggy and losses consciousness, remaining asleep for 3d20 minutes or until they are forcefully awakened. 3, The drinker finds the tea entirely flavorless, tasting only boiled water and experiences no other effects. 4, Over the course of the next minute, the drinker grows a thick black uni-brow and mustache, both of which remain until shaved off or otherwise removed or spontaneously falls out after 1d4 weeks. 5, The drinker’s voice drops so low it sounds like a growl that lasts for 1d4 hours. 6, All of the hair below the drinker’s neck instantly falls out, but will regrow normally.
3519. A floating spherical chess board that when opened, reveals intricately crafted pieces inside it. The pieces magically adhere to the sphere as it floats, and allows you to play without the chessmen falling off.
3520. A floor tile with a footprint on it that reads: “Step here to summon Balog.” The tile is slightly charred.
3521. A flower made of contorted brass, that appears to have been excellently handcrafted in the likeness of a daisy.
3522. A flowing river contained within a glass case
3523. A flute carved from brilliant white ash
3524. A flute carved from petrified wood in the shape of a skull that when played, causes all who listen to it feel uneasy.
3525. A flute carved from rich-colored wood
3526. A flute made of coarse unfinished bamboo, engraved around its circumference with unreadable sigils. A red silken cord is tied around it just below the mouthpiece. The instrument’s songs are always soothing, resting the weary minds of those who listened to its melodious tones.
3527. A flute that makes no sound, no matter how it is played. It’s surface is a shimmery grey.
3528. A flute that makes only the sound of a fog-horn, in various pitches, not necessarily in relation to the normal rules concerning depth of tone in a wind instrument.
3529. A flute whose out-of-tune notes can play no known song.
3530. A flute, tuned to an odd key, made from bone of an unknown creature.
3531. A fluted crystal vase, 18 inches in height.
3532. A folded parchment containing the notes to a whimsical tune.
3533. A folded piece of parchment reveals a charcoal sketch of a stunning young woman. A wide lake and a large tree split by lightning are depicted behind her
3534. A folding black leather case, containing half a dozen razor sharp obsidian scalpels.
3535. A folding compact shovel made from unusual blue metal.
3536. A folding knife with a curved tang, like a straight razor, but with a wickedly hooked blade. When closed, it forms a semicircle. The blade is quite pitted but has been cleaned of its rust at some point. It is keenly sharp.
3537. A folding leather wallet filled with strangely coloured bits of paper. Tucked within, you also find a small pewter badge showing the symbol of a great wyrm atop a castle wall, with the initials ‘GG’ on its reverse.
3538. A folding pocket knife
3539. A foot long twisted piece of driftwood carved into a crude idol. A perceptive PC can make out the hints of a screaming face among the cracks and whorls of its surface.
3540. A foot long wand made from tempered glass that can only serve as a focus for fire based spells.
3541. A foot long wand made of green jade etched with images of guardian lions fighting a species of demon known as the Oni. The wand currently possess no magical properties but could be enchanted with some by those with the talent.
3542. A foot long, iridescent feather that gently flutters even when there is no wind. Knowledgeable PC’s will recognize this supple object as originating from a winged Coatl.
3543. A foot stool with silver-plated eagle claw feet and silken pillow.
3544. A foot-long black periscope made primary of steel
3545. A foot-long rod made entirely of zinc
3546. A foot-long, light blue candle with golden patterning. When burned the candle smells of ozone and creates harmless sparks of static electricity.
3547. A forest elf’s rucksack that is simply the treated husk of a giant seedpod, fitted with leather strap hinges and closed with buckled leather straps. The long, organic vessel is hard-sided and durable, with naturally formed compartments inside.
3548. A forest green cloak clasp, engraved with a stylized version of a tree.
3549. A forest of tiny bright red crystals emerging from an uneven brown stone that resembles soil. Knowledgeable PC’s can identify the mineral as crocoite.
3550. A forgery kit that contains a variety of papers, parchments, pens and inks, seals, sealing wax, gold and silver leaf, and other supplies necessary to create convincing forgeries of physical documents.
3551. A forked darkwood wand with a tip of beech, ending in an image of a woman’s face, with real alchemically preserved, shrunken eyes.
3552. A fortune-telling stone with a two closed, feminine eyes.
3553. A fossilized ammonite, fashioned into a necklace.
3554. A fossilized curled shell that sometimes uncurls
3555. A fossilized egg that more closely resembles obsidian than shell. A feeling of unsettling dread nags at the thoughts of any creature who holds it.
3556. A fossilized heart of an unknown creature. Those who hold it hear a slow but steady beat within their head.
3557. A foul collar sized for a human or a huge dog that is studded with spikes on the inside and out. Made from interlinked pieces of iron and brass, the spikes are covered in old blood and bits of skin and hair.
3558. A four foot long iron pry bar
3559. A four foot long rod capped at each end by a six-inch-wide band of gold and steel. The rod has a three-foot long section of clear crystal in the middle, filled with a swirling white fog.
3560. A four high wireframe model of a humanoid figure, made out of tin. The figure has an exquisitely detailed copper heart inside the dull ribcage.
3561. A four inch long, plain, pocket pen carved from giant’s tooth enamel, tipped with a gold-vanadium nib, and silver cap.
3562. A four-foot long grand tapestry with flour along the bottom, depicting the steps in which to make a pie. On the back, is a recipe written in an unknown language
3563. A four-leaf clover pressed inside a book discussing manners and etiquette
3564. A fragile black paper fan. When unfolded, the fan depicts two mesmerizing cat’s eyes.
3565. A fragile, half empty bottle of a rare perfume.
3566. A fragment of a beautiful song, written as musical notes on two pieces of parchment
3567. A fragment of a painting torn from a larger canvas depicting an unfamiliar princess.
3568. A fragment of bone, obviously the concave top plate of a human cranium, edged with pitted gold. Three inches wide and carved on the inside with cramped script. A reader adept in Abyssal will discern an outdated version of a prayer to the Dark Lord of Random Evil Domain carved within.
3569. A framed painting of an empty field in. As time goes, the picture changes and adds things like people, livestock and houses to itself.
3570. A framed sheet of glass that appears to show a crystal-clear picture of the current surrounding area from a high vantage, but it is always completely wrong.
3571. A freshly dead messenger raven with a tiny scroll tube tied to its leg. Within is a small parchment with some sort of coded battle report written on it.
3572. A freshly-excavated humanoid skeleton the size of a hand
3573. A frog figurine carved from ivory
3574. A frost-covered silver locket that’s frozen shut and never thaws.
3575. A frozen, crystalline gland from some unknown ancient being. Hard as stone, it thaws slowly but eternally. The alien object is nearly translucent, revealing a void filled with nothing but bright, cold light. The glowing core holds a strange allure, turning the mind toward rapturous reminiscence.
3576. A full deck of playing cards each of which has two backs and no faces.
3577. A full face mask made of beech, which when worn, causes the bearer’s voice to become two octaves higher.
3578. A full face mask made of oak, which when worn, causes the bearer’s voice to become two octaves deeper.
3579. A full faced, black velvet mask stitched with silver thread
3580. A full mask of rippled black glass with thin slanted slits for the bearer’s eyes. All who look upon it see distorted reflections of their own faults and failings, the slightest doubt twisted into a horrific swarm of phantoms that claw at the psyche of the victim. The bearer is never affected by the mask’s powers but longtime users may find their sense of compassion and empathy withering away the longer they stare out of the dark veil’s slanted eye slits.
3581. A fully function violin made of the jawbone of a large animal of unknown origin.
3582. A fully functioning clarinet carved from driftwood. The holy symbol of a minor lake deity is branded into the side
3583. A fully functioning xylophone made out of Giant’s toe nails.
3584. A functional lute made entirely out of dry grass.
3585. A funeral urn made of fine porcelain some two feet high.
3586. A funeral urn sealed with a stopper depicting a golden-haired jackal.
3587. A fuzzy stuffed bear that makes growling noises when you squeeze it
3588. A fuzzy toy animal that seems to repel any dirt.
3589. A gallon cask filled with yellow clothiers’ dye.
3590. A gallon jug of thick smoky glass wrapped in braided twine. The container is filled with a potent liquor strong enough to strip paint from wood. Only the eldest brigands of the wildlands know the secret to distilling a libation so pure. The devout have no need of drink, but vagabonds always thirst for more. One who consumes this superior moonshine feels they can take on the world and is filled with resolve.
3591. A gallon of rum in a small wooden keg
3592. A game of dominoes in a small leather case
3593. A garish tabard made from simple red cloth, with gold-coloured trim that has frayed badly. The effect of the wear is such that the tabard’s edge appears to be fluffy; the strands of cheap gold fabric float like tentacles in the slightest breeze. The front of the tabard is dominated by a somewhat-successfully stitched image depicting a drop of blood. The back is adorned with a single letter “I” and has undergone the same transformation as the gold trim. Donning the tabard causes several wayward strands to drift upward into the bearer’s face.
3594. A garland made of the last fallen leaves of the season.
3595. A gaudy purse containing items to assist a courtesan in soothing the body and mind. For the body, the kit contains a razor, scented oils and salves, fragrances and a few wisps of silk that is probably meant to serve as “clothing”. It also includes a book of poetry, focusing on salacious topics and full of double entendres, to entertain the mind.
3596. A gemstone that looks like a lump of coal when examined by anyone but you
3597. A gemstone that takes on the appearance of the birth stone of the last person to touch it.
3598. A genuine looking, but harmless (Blunted) stage dagger with a retracting blade and blood-compartment.
3599. A geologist’s rock hammer consisting of a sharp pick head and a flat blunted head made entirely of steel.
3600. A giant beetle carapace of iridescent blues, purples, and greens. A talented armorer could make this into a large shield or breastplate.
3601. A gigantic snake’s tooth that has an unsettling aura about it.
3602. A gilded acorn containing a feather, a tuft of fur and a fish tail.
3603. A gilded oil lamp with ornate scrollwork cutouts of a warrior, and an evil sorcerer, meant to cast shadows on the wall in their shape.
3604. A gilded puzzle box decorated with a asymmetrical geometric pattern.
3605. A gilded robin’s egg.
3606. A gilded teacup, laced with a spiderweb of thin cracks. Despite the cracks, it never seems to break.
3607. A gilded wineglass fashioned from a human skull and set with lapis lazuli.
3608. A glass ball filled with fluid through which swims a bloodshot eye trailing its nerve endings
3609. A glass bauble with no visible means of opening it contains blue sand and white insects that resemble ants but have iridescent wings. When they fly, a pleasant and soothing song emanates from the bauble.
3610. A glass bead that glows faintly, but not enough to see or read by.
3611. A glass bottle of some type of potent-smelling tonic. It’s taste is gritty and bitter, and somewhat reminiscent of charcoal.
3612. A glass bottle that glitters like gold.
3613. A glass cube that causes light moving through it to act strangely, forming square patterns of blue and purple colors.
3614. A glass cube that is always cold
3615. A glass cube that replays in graphic detail the bearer’s worst memory when held for longer than one minute.
3616. A glass cube that shows what seems to be a live aerial view of an unknown, ruined city
3617. A glass cube with a preserved eye in the center
3618. A glass disk that depict constellations.
3619. A glass disk that displays images of a different plane.
3620. A glass doorknob taken from a haunted house, that occasionally lets out pained moaning noises.
3621. A glass eye
3622. A glass eye with an azurite iris and obsidian pupil.
3623. A glass eyeball that is half as large as a human eye
3624. A glass eyeball that is three times larger than a human eye
3625. A glass figurine of a unicorn with a chalcedony horn.
3626. A glass flask encased in cold iron bands
3627. A glass flask messily labelled “Alchemist’s Fire”. It actually contains a highly-potent cinnamon whisky.
3628. A glass flask with a loose paper label marked, “Captain’s 'Special’ Rum Cocktail”. It smells and tastes like a mix of Rum, ginger and honey. Exactly what makes it special, is not clear.
3629. A glass flute that produces no sound
3630. A glass globe of swirling green goop with no openings.
3631. A glass globe that has a winged being dancing on the head of a pin within it.
3632. A glass globe that has no visible opening on its dark butted surface, and it is warm to the touch. Its contents appear to be a faintly glowing roiling butt of flame.
3633. A glass hookah with a cap made of gold, its hose of tightly woven silk, and its mouth piece is cunningly carved from a piece of amber that contains an entire tiny scorpion. Even when the hookah is not lit, the interior of its glass body is hazy, as if with smoke.
3634. A glass jar containing a harmless clear gel, the jar is labelled in dwarvish as ‘Gelatinous Cube – Deceased.’
3635. A glass jar containing a heart that continues to beat.
3636. A glass jar containing a number of preserved tentacles of an unidentifiable creature. There are six, connected around what appears to be a mouth.
3637. A glass jar containing a two-headed cat fetus
3638. A glass jar containing a weird bit of flesh floating in pickling fluid
3639. A glass jar containing an ectoplasmic substance that glows faintly in moonlight.
3640. A glass jar containing lard with a label that reads, “Griffon Grease”
3641. A glass jar containing orange and red fruit preserves. A rough and jolly swashbuckler is imprinted on the lid. Along the side is written “ Jelly Roger’s Marmalade: To prevent scurvy on the high seas!”
3642. A glass jar containing six eyeballs (2 human, 1 goblin, 1 ogre, 1 warg, and 1 hawk) in a clear embalming liquid.
3643. A glass jar containing the preserved ear of a night hag in embalming fluid. If exposed to starlight, it causes those nearby to hear the faint sound of children wailing
3644. A glass jar containing two eyeballs peering out with innocence. Its label reads “Lithians treasure” scribbled in crayon.
3645. A glass jar filled with clippings of dwarven hair and toenails.
3646. A glass jar filled with humanoid baby teeth, marked “For emergency use only!”.
3647. A glass jar filled with the teeth of children
3648. A glass jar filled with vinegar and a dismembered troll finger that twitches occasionally. It seems like the weak acid from the vinegar is keeping the finger in a sort of stasis. You imagine that if it was removed it might fully regenerate…
3649. A glass jar full of pickled eyes
3650. A glass jar full of weightless sand.
3651. A glass jar that automatically separates any liquid poured into it into multiple layers of individual substances, as though a centrifuge had been employed.
3652. A glass jar, blue, sagging with the slow melt of ages. Within are three seeds, somehow not yet turned to dust. They are like those of a pumpkin, or squash, but are reddish and much pointier.
3653. A glass lighting bolt, two inches long that makes one’s hair stand on end if touched
3654. A glass marble that looks a bit like a lizard’s eye and is always a bit cold to the touch.
3655. A glass orb filled with moving smoke
3656. A glass orb filled with water, in which swims a clockwork goldfish
3657. A glass plate that shows an aerial view of a city that no one’s ever seen.
3658. A glass plate that shows what seems to be a live image of the moon, but from a closer vantage
3659. A glass shell that sometimes has the ghostly visage of a crab inside it
3660. A glass skull wrapped in taut, tanned humanoid leather.
3661. A glass tube of purple smoke that changes to a liquid when exposed to sunlight
3662. A glass vase hand painted in bright colors, depicting a thriving desert scene with several exotic succulents, cacti, and flowers on it.
3663. A glass vial filled with nail clippings
3664. A glassy charm in the shape of a moon, with many claw and tooth marks.
3665. A glazed porcelain pipe. Everything about it seems vaguely familiar, but you aren’t sure why.
3666. A gleaming crystal shard that shifts its coloration every few seconds, hurting the eyes of witnesses as it does so.
3667. A gleaming turtle shell that calls out to be carved into something lovely.
3668. A glob of chewing putty that never loses its flavor or texture
3669. A glossy black hunting horn, chased with runes and knotwork of silver.
3670. A glossy cowrie shell in the shape of a crescent moon, incorporated into a hairpin. Knowledgeable PC’s will know that these shells are sometimes claimed as natural wards against lycanthropes.
3671. A glowing glass globe that, if taken underwater, is never moved or disturbed by currents
3672. A glowing orb that has a hidden button on it. Pressing it reveals several smaller variously colored orbs inside, which escape the orb and start to orbit it, like planets around a star. Pressing the button again causes the spheres to retreat back into the glowing one.
3673. A glowing teal line, six inches long, but with no discernible radius
3674. A gnarled pipe smells strongly of cinnamon and fish, disturbing your digestion. Its bowl has constellations etched around it.
3675. A gnome sized pair of spectacles. The glass lenses are in the shape of gears and each are stained in a different Bright Colors.
3676. A gnome sized silver hair brush. On it in Orcish script is crudely scratched “Remember Me”
3677. A Gnome’s skull that possesses a distinct odour of wine and gnome blood, faint to a human, but strong to a race with keen noses, like elves or kobolds. Rough garnets the color of clotted blood have been pounded into the skull, along with nails of silver and gold. Around lower edges of the brain-case, the tails of giant weasels have been attached, giving the item a furry fringe. The jaw has been wired to the skull with silver wire and a wispy fringe of beard and mustache cling to the bits of dried skin around the skull’s mouth. Runes of foul power have been chiselled into the bone.
3678. A goat shaped idol, fashioned from the horn of the animal that it resembles.
3679. A goblet carved from blackened bone and ivory with black adamantium fittings. It is the size of a large brazier, its basin in the shape of a toothed demon face, its base a nest of serpents.
3680. A goblet made from clear glass infused with powdered black jade.
3681. A goblet, when filled with clear liquid, reveals at the bottom of the cup an image of a sea floor with a treasure chest overflowing with gold coins.
3682. A goblin’s skull with treasure map crudely carved into it. Knowledgeable PCs will recognize local landmarks on the map.
3683. A gold coin minted in an unknown land
3684. A gold coin of strange design, one one side of the coin are two crossed-swords and on the other a bulbous eye that appears to blink occasionally.
3685. A gold coin that always comes up heads when flipped.
3686. A gold coin that when flipped remains suspended in the air, spinning indefinitely until it is grabbed again.
3687. A gold colored pill box depicting a single cat on the lid.
3688. A gold colored, weightless, egg shaped object.
3689. A gold eye-patch set with three red spinels, on a black leather cord. The back of the eye-patch bears a stylized shadow in black lacquer, the symbol of The Dark, a large criminal organization that has hands in everything from drugs and prostitution to assassinations.
3690. A gold monocle frame without the lens
3691. A gold piece that has been bitten in half.
3692. A gold plated compass with cracked crystal in a small teak box carved with waves.
3693. A gold rimmed monocle with light rope of gold and clip. The glass of the monocle is smudged and butty but resists all attempts at cleaning.
3694. A gold-plated badge emblazoned with a symbol of fire
3695. A golden cloak clasp in a pattern of knotted vines covered in small leaves.
3696. A golden, translucent bracelet made of a lightweight, silky material that resembles warm amber. In fact, small creatures can be seen trapped within. If watched closely, they seem to move.
3697. A good luck charm bracelet made from the teeth of a giant lizard.
3698. A good luck charm made from the skulls and bones of three small corvids tied together by leather cord. Each has a symbol painted on them - the symbols are life, death, and fortune.
3699. A gorgerine of alternating bone and metal discs aligned in columns that ascend in size from top to bottom.
3700. A gray ingot of unknown metal, with an oily purple sheen and unnatural spiraling engravings on its surface.
3701. A green bottle enchanted to deliver a heartfelt message. When opened the bottle emits the voice of a crying man speaking to his wife, saying how sorry he is and how nobody should go through the Duskfall Forest. The message will play over and over again until the bottle is closed.
3702. A green leather pouch full of berries that never rot
3703. A green velvet bag containing a magically preserved daisy chain.
3704. A green wig, made from dyed horse hair
3705. A green, glass apple barely skinned with flaking gold leaf. Within, there are visible countless rivers of incredibly tiny veins, fibers, and seeds, as if a real apple were refashioned precisely as glass. Said seeds are glimmering red, perhaps rubies. The apple is profoundly strong and cannot easily break.
3706. A green, metal orb that slowly orbits any obese humanoid it’s thrown at.
3707. A grey glass bottle labeled “Fungus Wine”, sealed with a large cork carved into the shape of a mushroom cap.
3708. A grey leather vest is made of the rough, tanned hide of a goblin shark.
3709. A griffin’s beak strung on a leather cord.
3710. A grossly oversized fist-shaped gauntlet that is a fused amalgamation of plates, spurs, gears, and rivets. It smells of grease and machinery.
3711. A grotesque drum carved from old bones and covered with the stretched skin of flayed vampires.
3712. A gruesome hand fan made of plucked faerie wings
3713. A gruesome lantern made from bone and emerald-tinted glass. When lit, it sheds a disgusting green, dim light that makes living creatures uncomfortable, but does them no harm.
3714. A gruesome war banner sewn from strings of congealed gore, the flag radiates bloodlust so strong that those beneath it are filled with rage.
3715. A guitar that, regardless of what string is plucked, will always produce the same set of notes in the same order. Only the speed at which these notes are produced can be changed.
3716. A hair pin made of amethyst, shaped like an axe.
3717. A hairpin made of a deep red copper, fashioned into the shape of a lily flower.
3718. A hairpin with head shaped like a spider and set with red agate.
3719. A hairwork needlepoint displayed under a cabochon of greenish glass. Mounted on a nickel silver brooch. The needlepoint depicts, in black and blond, presumably-human, hair, a smiling black skeleton beckoning to a quizzical, robed child.
3720. A half dozen clay dolls that are said to hold the spirits of dead warriors from a mystic culture. It is said that the spirits will haunt any person that destroys the dolls. A faint whistling can be heard from the clay dolls whenever they are placed outside at night.
3721. A half dozen shrunken heads that have been partially eaten, all strung on a braided hemp rope. Knowledgeable PC’s will have heard rumors of a local cannibalistic cult that was active generations ago but it is thought that they killed themselves off, as the number of unexplained disappearances dropped over the years. The cult used abominable practices to shrink the heads of their victims to serve as offerings to their dark God.
3722. A half empty pepper grinder
3723. A half-mask like one worn to a masquerade party, but the bottom edge has numerous sharp-looking catlike teeth.
3724. A hamster skeleton that animates and dances whenever music is played. The undead rodent moves to the beat and is disturbingly cute to watch.
3725. A hand carved wooden box filled with tobacco
3726. A hand fan made of stretched aquatic elf skin decorated in horn and painted with animal scenes of hunger.
3727. A hand mirror backed with a bronze depiction of a gorgon
3728. A hand mirror crafted with a pewter handle and backing with intricate engraving. The reflective surface seems to be a silvery metal that never burnishes or scratches and all reflections appear dimmed and shadowy.
3729. A hand mirror made of polished black obsidian that shows all those who glance into it in monochrome.
3730. A hand mirror that cracks when someone smiles in it, and fixes itself when someone grimaces in it
3731. A hand mirror that only shows what the bearer looks like from behind.
3732. A hand mirror that reflects your face exactly as it looked yesterday
3733. A hand mirror that shows the reflected creature aging rapidly. If looked at long enough, the creature’s reflection will die of old age, rot and decay into dust.
3734. A hand mirror which displays creatures reflected in it as crying inconsolably.
3735. A hand mirror with a butterfly relief on it’s non-mirror side while it’s reflective surface appears to be a pool of moonlight.
3736. A hand mirror, whose semisolid handle and frame are formed of flowing liquid shadow.
3737. A hand puppet that strongly resembles one of the PC’s
3738. A hand sized ball of parchment tied up with string. If the string is removed, and the many parchment layers unwrapped, at the center is a pinch of bright yellow sulfur.
3739. A hand sized bell with no clapper that still rings
3740. A hand sized blue figurine of a woman dressed in an oddly cut skirt and coat.
3741. A hand sized branding iron. The brand matches the seal of a nearby archmage who has an unproven reputation for cruelty.
3742. A hand sized brass miniature of a ship
3743. A hand sized brass peacock with ruby eyes and feathers made of colored glass.
3744. A hand sized bronze statuette of a huntress with dogs at her feet. One eye socket is vacant and the other contains a glimmering opal. Touching the statue makes the user see strange lands with their left eye as the statuette’s mouth opens and closes.
3745. A hand sized fang that fell naturally from a spider dragon’s maw.
3746. A hand sized figurine made of bronze, depicting a stout man with a beard holding a book. The man bears a merry smile.
3747. A hand sized figurine made of glass, of a man raising his hand as if to signal stop.
3748. A hand sized figurine made of highly polished garnet, carved into the shape of two red dragon whelps locked in combat.
3749. A hand sized figurine of a lighthouse made of smoked grey glass, with a piece of cut quartz serving as a beacon.
3750. A hand sized figurine of a little fat man sitting cross-legged with a sword across his knees. It is carved of shiny dark green stone.
3751. A hand sized figurine of a soldier, complete with detachable metal armor.
3752. A hand sized figurine of a spiraling wizard’s tower made of red glass.
3753. A hand sized figurine of an unclothed woman made of alabaster.
3754. A hand sized obsidian box in the shape of a mountain, containing volcanic ash.
3755. A hand sized preserved skeleton that seems to be half man and half sea-lion.
3756. A hand sized sea foam colored scale from a sea serpent. It feels hard and rough to the touch and weighs as much as iron but floats like cork.
3757. A hand sized statuette made of otherworldly black metal, depicting a tentacled, beaked horror.
3758. A hand sized statuette of a fire elemental. It appears to be made from a still burning hunk of wood that was never put out. Embers still crackle and burn in the in the wood, the objects and the air around the statuette shimmering as if it was very hot. Although the object does feel warm to the touch, the embers never ignite anything or consume the statuette.
3759. A hand sized statuette of a water elemental. It seems to be constructed primarily of a piece of driftwood embedded with seashell shards and large rough pieces of sea salt with two pearls making up its eyes. While holding the statuette a creature can hears the sounds of the ocean and smells fresh sea air.
3760. A hand sized statuette of a white porcelain elephant with real ivory tusks
3761. A hand sized statuette of a wooden zebra. No creature is able to determine if it’s black with white stripes or white with black stripes.
3762. A hand sized statuette of an air elemental. It is made primarily of hollowed glass with holes and slits in various places to take in and channel airflow. Tiny down feathers are dotted inside and out, constantly being blown in various directions regardless of the actual airflow in the area.
3763. A hand sized statuette of an angel made entirely out of razor blades and barbed wire. There is no safe place to grab or hold the figure.
3764. A hand sized statuette of an earth elemental. It is made primarily of clay of with small obsidian spiked teeth and black onyx eyes. The clay is perpetually damp and malleable allowing for minor adjustment to the statuette’s posture.
3765. A hand sized totem carved from cooled lava pulled from the Great Burning Sea.
3766. A hand sized white marble statuette of an angel weeping into it’s hands. When no one is watching, it occasionally moves to a nearby location or to a different pouch or bag.
3767. A hand sized wooden coffin filled with grave earth
3768. A hand sized, grey, stone statuette of a woman. It’s exceptionally detailed for its size, as even the folds in her cloak look almost lifelike.
3769. A hand sized, silly looking humanoid statue that changes what direction it’s pointing at unknown times.
3770. A hand sized, statuette of an armoured warrior made of a dark, shiny metal.
3771. A hand sized, wooden statuette of a lanky humanoid in a fetal position. Its androgynous face bears a terrified expression.
3772. A hand-mirror that sometimes shows a shadowy figure lurking behind the person looking in it.
3773. A hand-sized figurine of a crystalline scorpion that is so full of hairline cracks that it looks as though it could fall apart at any second. Despite its appearance that object is actually quite durable.
3774. A hand-sized ivory carving of a seal that floats to the surface when dropped into any liquid.
3775. A hand-sized wooden statuette of a black panther
3776. A handful of bones of an extinct creature that was said to have been immune to magic. The creature was commonly thought to be something of legend and not actually real. It was said that magical power not only had no hold over the creature, but they even dampened magical effects around them.
3777. A handful of carefully folded pages ripped out of an accounting journal of a local merchant.
3778. A handful of fine gold dust wrapped up in a parchment sealed with red wax.
3779. A handful of jasper puzzle pieces speckled with flecks of semiprecious stones (Citrine, amethyst, garnet, etc.) that can be assembled into the likeness of a bird of prey.
3780. A handful of polished dice carved from bleached human knucklebones. One of the dice is obviously heavier than the rest.
3781. A handful of small metal tiles that taste good but are inedible
3782. A handful of small, silver and copper coins pressed with unfamiliar faces and strange lettering.
3783. A handheld chisel that scratches only wooden materials. If used on dirt or rock, it softens in your hands.
3784. A handheld fan with lightweight steel leaves. When fully opened on its creaking rivet, it forms three quarters of a circle. The leaves are spotted with delicate openwork in flowering geometric patterns, some of which have been eaten through by rust.
3785. A handheld mirror that’s cracked with one missing shard of glass. Each new moon, the missing shard appears, and voices singing unsettling songs emanate from it until the sun rises.
3786. A handheld mirror with a cobalt border engraved with strange runes. Instead of your reflection, you see nothing but mist in its surface.
3787. A handheld sounding post-horn. The instrument has detailed, graphic engravings of humanoids being torn asunder by sword and axe. A single blow of this horn will surely strike fear in any tribe or group that has the misfortune of being a target for conquest.
3788. A handwritten note which reads, in part: “She keeps it in the vanity. Without it, there’s no proof.”
3789. A hangman’s noose that feels heavier than it should, as if weighed down with misery
3790. A hard black leather case containing a flawless set of well-used professional kitchen knives, one nearly as long as your arm.
3791. A hard-bristled tool commonly referred to as a dandy brush. It’s handle slips over the bearer’s hand, and is used by animal breeders, owners, and grooms to brush the animals in their care, loosening dirt and detritus from the creatures’ coats and stimulating the skin so it produces the natural oils that keep them healthy and clean.
3792. A hard, roughly cut piece of basalt rock which bleeds when pressure is applied to it.
3793. A hardy wine bottle that is completely opaque and holds a strong, deep red, fruity alcohol.
3794. A hardy, darkened conch shell with an almost dangerous amount of ridges and points. By holding the shell up to their ear, the holder can hear rushing water and violent waves with an unusual degree of clarity. Continuing to listen the shell causes the bearer to slowly begin to experience sensations of seasickness and a pressure similar to being too deep underwater. As the sensations intensify, a muted, indecipherable whispering can be heard very faintly, as if it were coming from the bottom of the sea. The whispering continues to grow louder while the sensations escalate, until the whispers can almost be made out, at which point the bearer blacks out for a few seconds, dropping the shell.
3795. A harlequin mask decorated with carbuncles on the forehead, small eyes, a snub nose, hollow cheeks, and bushy brows with facial hair.
3796. A harlequin mask that makes the bearer feel oddly sad whenever worn.
3797. A hastella, six inches tall, made of fragile wood. Defaced with profane graffiti deeply scored with a pointed knife. The rude letters belong to a language now forgotten. They have since been filled with some silvery metal, and their edges now rise higher than the worn-down wood.
3798. A hat that rests just on top of the bearer’s head but never blows or falls off
3799. A hauntingly terrifying mask that appears to have be fashioned from leather, metal and nightmares.
3800. A hawthorn walking stick. The end stained with mud, and worn from use from walking through cities, deserts, moors, up mountains, and from being used to pry open many windows, and doors.
3801. A head sized array of complicated clockwork mechanisms that throb slightly as pulsing with an organic heartbeat.
3802. A head sized leather ball stuffed with chicken feathers
3803. A head-sized dodecahedron; bronze and blackened. Each of the twelve sides has a wide hole in its center. Visible through the holes is the indistinct form of a lead-dipped skull, evidently entrapped there when the hedron was forged.
3804. A headband made of crimson cloth with an ancient symbol in its center; A disc with a sinuous line separating a white side from a black side.
3805. A headband woven from magically preserved holly, its deep green leaves and bright red berries are perpetually lustrous.
3806. A heady, sweet smelling noose made of still-living flowers and freshly cut vines.
3807. A heart-shaped smoking pipe
3808. A heartfelt poem about unrequited love on a pristine scroll.
3809. A heavily embellished accordion. A bearer with a sharp eye will notice that the leather on it is actually tanned human lung and the keys are polished bone.
3810. A heavily weathered, training greatsword. The greatsword is a heavily weighted, wooden practice weapon, used as a way to build endurance and expertise. It weighs twice as much as a normal one would (It contains a lead filled core), causing it to be almost unusable at first.
3811. A heavily weathered, training longsword. The longsword is a heavily weighted, wooden practice weapon, used as a way to build endurance and expertise. It weighs twice as much as a normal one would (It contains a lead filled core), causing it to be somewhat unwieldy at first.
3812. A heavily worn and stained soft leather butcher’s apron. The front bears a pyrographic skull within a runed circle.
3813. A heavy brass torc ending in clenched fists.
3814. A heavy builder’s mallet with a wooden shaft and a head made from shiny black iron that turns slightly red when held close to metal.
3815. A heavy copper belt buckle resembling an axe head.
3816. A heavy dual thickness pouch that seems to glow slightly. Inside, are a dozen or so tiny winged beetles. The faint glow emanates from their wings. If the pouch is left open, they fly away.
3817. A heavy eight inch gate key, fashioned of cold iron, inscribed with hieroglyphs from an earlier age. If translated, the hieroglyphs translate to “Star Door”.
3818. A heavy glass heart that is perfectly to scale with a human heart. The object is crafted of dark, smoky glass and has a hole for dipping in the gaping atrium that is filled with the gummy remnants of old, reddish resin, perhaps ink.
3819. A heavy granite flute of dwarven design.
3820. A heavy iron bell inscribed with distorted musical symbols that releases an incredibly cacophonous noise when rung.
3821. A heavy iron coin that floats on water
3822. A heavy iron mask, intricately carved patterns and runes.
3823. A heavy key to the ancestral crypt of an influential noble family.
3824. A heavy lead disk that depicts writhing tentacles and widespread destruction. The rim of the disk has the word “NEVINYRRAL” on it.
3825. A heavy scroll case made of solid iron.
3826. A heavy wooden boomerang that could serve as a decent weapon or hunting tool in the hands of a skilled wielder.
3827. A heavy wooden box containing several dozen black and white, square ceramic mosaic tiles.
3828. A heavy wooden box containing several dozen Randomly Coloured, square ceramic mosaic tiles.
3829. A heavy, bronze mask that lets out a deep, sonorous ring when struck.
3830. A heavy, red silk sash with an ornate chain pattern embroidered on both sides.
3831. A heavy, rust-covered iron rod that whispers unintelligible words only the wielder can hear
3832. A herbalism kit that contains a variety of instruments such as clippers, mortar and pestle, and pouches and vials used by herbalists to create remedies and potions.
3833. A hessian sack full of bird feathers in the most fantastic colours imaginable.
3834. A hickory cane with a polished rose gold head in the shape of a grinning head.
3835. A high quality smoking pipe made of marble.
3836. A high-crowned, wide-brimmed hat made of black felt. This style of hat is typically worn by cattle ranchers and is commonly referred to as a “ten gallon hat”.
3837. A high-crowned, wide-brimmed hat made of white felt. This style of hat is typically worn by cattle ranchers and is commonly referred to as a “ten gallon hat”.
3838. A high, conical mask, intricately decorated with dried corn kernels of different colours.
3839. A highly polished shell horn made of walrus tusk.
3840. A hilt from a broken sword
3841. A hilt of what once must have been a magnificent sword but the blade has been removed. When the hilt is picked up, the wielder feels the weight of a whole sword and when the hilt is whipped around, the wielder can hear a blade slicing through the air. But it is just a hilt…
3842. A hollow bronze tube that emits a resounding chime when struck against a hard surface. The chime has an elaborate handle resembling an angel whose wings surround it.
3843. A hollow glass sphere roughly the size of a fist containing a colorful fog. If it is broken, those nearby will catch the barest scent of alien perfumes, hinting at strange lands belonging to the ancient time when the sphere was first forged and air trapped within it.
3844. A hollow globe depicting the planet, that hangs over a small brass candle stand and chain. Wax covers the small brass candle stand.
3845. A hollow iron sphere, inscribed with an eye-like iris, and a brass hinge and clasp allow it to open.
3846. A hollow, seamless, clear quarts cube with unknown beasts carved in negative into the inside.
3847. A hollowed-out hen’s egg that squawks loudly when broken and immediately puts itself back together.
3848. A hollowed, curled demon horn. When blown, it sounds like tortured screaming.
3849. A holy symbol of a church that preaches celibacy that was carved from the bone of a vestal virgin.
3850. A holy symbol of a long forgotten god, carved from bone
3851. A holy symbol of a powerful religion, that is covered in soot that resists all efforts to clean off.
3852. A honeycomb intricately carved from marble and polished to a fine finish.
3853. A hooded lantern that is painted rather intricately with a house cat motif depicting cats playing in different poses. It’s mostly painted in whites and pinks, and it has small scuff marks on one side.
3854. A hooded lantern with an adjustable iris to control the light level. Its adamantine casing is covered in stars and concentric circles, as well as text no one seems able to read.
3855. A horn hair brush inlaid with small peridot stones.
3856. A horned and fanged skull that looks human.
3857. A horrendously ugly shirt which cleans and folds itself when removed.
3858. A horrid war banner that seems to be little more than a leather rag on a totem pole, but closer inspection reveals foul stains that drip and splash as the bearer moves. The whole of the vile hide throbs and oozes a greenish tinted fluid.
3859. A horrific black mask carved in the likeness of a demon’s face. Massive curved horns sweep up and back out of the forehead and behind the ears, while the fangs seem to glisten as if ready to bite at any moment. When worn, the mask’s eye sockets become covered with a glassy shield that glows red. When the bearer speaks, his voice is broadcast as a guttural growl.
3860. A horse femur that is as light as a feather.
3861. A horse-shaped bottle, made of iron, with eagles of gold and genuine lapis lazuli as inlays.
3862. A horseshoe that never becomes worn or needs replacing.
3863. A hourglass with blue and orange sand, that has been frozen in time.
3864. A human femur covered with detailed etching in an unknown language.
3865. A human femur made of glass
3866. A human mandible with strange, silvery crystals jutting in a painful major from the ramus and processes, where it might connect to the skull. The teeth, of which the incisors are sharp, are faintly coated in platinum leaf.
3867. A human skull and the ball joint end of a femur which have been altered into a mortar and pestle.
3868. A human skull goblet with silver base.
3869. A human skull with elongated canine teeth.
3870. A human skull, yellowed with age, with the jawbone wired in place with fine copper wire. The skull has been etched with very fine runes, visible only in bright light, that identify it with the name of “Jerimr”. When the skull’s name is spoken aloud by someone holding it in their left hand, it will rise into the air and assume a position over his left shoulder, hovering unsupported.
3871. A human-faced insect trapped in a piece of amber
3872. A humanoid eyeball encased in crystal
3873. A humorous ensorcelled cartoon strip about Cernuous Cedric the slug-about-town, a languorous libertine known for his lechery, taste for strong drink, and allergy to any form of labor. The strip speaks and animates when read, telling the story of one of Cedric’s disastrous affairs with the husband of Mordiggia, the Charnel Goddess.
3874. A hunk of driftwood that smells of salt. It is incredibly malleable and can be bent by hand without damaging it.
3875. A hunk of metal that is full of holes. The holes collect and hold fluid, despite the fact that they go all the way through the material.
3876. A hunk of metal which appears to be several gears jammed together at unnatural and impossible angles: attempting to turn it causes it to emit a horrible shrieking sound
3877. A hunting trap that when set properly, forms a saw-toothed steel ring that snaps shut when a creature steps on the pressure plate in the center. The trap is affixed by a heavy chain, ending in a sharp spike that can can be wrapped around an immobile object or driven into the ground.
3878. A hunting trumpet made from hollowed out horn that plays a booming note which only canines can hear.
3879. A iridescent conch shell that when held to one’s ear, begs the creature to return it to the ocean.
3880. A iron belt buckle in the shape of a wolf’s head
3881. A iron blacksmith’s mold for crafting a dagger
3882. A iron blacksmith’s mold for crafting a pickaxe head
3883. A iron blacksmith’s mold for crafting a sickle
3884. A iron blacksmith’s mold for crafting a spearhead
3885. A iron blacksmith’s mold for crafting an ax-head
3886. A iron blacksmith’s mold for crafting arrowheads
3887. A iron blacksmith’s mold for crafting brass knuckles
3888. A iron blacksmith’s mold for crafting crossbow bolts
3889. A iron blacksmith’s mold for crafting shirkens
3890. A iron blacksmith’s mold for crafting sling bullets
3891. A iron blacksmith’s mold for crafting throwing knives
3892. A iron blacksmith’s mold for crafting war darts
3893. A jade carving of a flying fish, inexpertly done and with poor detailing.
3894. A jade mirror that exaggerates the features of those who looked into it in order to reveal their true nature through a sort of magical caricature. An honorable and virtuous person would appear stronger, taller, and more fair in visage. A selfish, greedy individual would appear smaller, with cruel, conniving features. Those with an injury or disability that they had allowed to cripple their spirit would appear withered and weak, while those who had overcome their disability would appear in perfect health.
3895. A jagged amber rob with a dead fairy sprite entombed within.
3896. A jagged shard of amethyst containing a mote of darkness.
3897. A jagged silver shard smeared with crimson blood.
3898. A jangling bracelet of tiny golden bells and fine-toothed cogs on crimson silk rope. It is suited to a small wrist interested in intricate mechanisms.
3899. A jar filled with potpourri that smells like their childhood home to each person who smells it.
3900. A jar made of cut crystal that is sealed with a stopper of iron. Its filled with amber fluid, and a floating clot of tissue that undulates and pulses.
3901. A jar of oil that, when spread on metal, makes the metal glow with a faint radiance for one hour
3902. A jar of putty that hardens after being exposed to air for one hour, but softens again if touched
3903. A jar of sand that seems to change color when looked at indirectly
3904. A jawbone from a mammalian beast, a handspan in length with dozens of tiny needle-like teeth arranged like a pincushion
3905. A jawbone of a donkey. All its teeth are black and anyone holding the jawbone experiences the slight flavor of rotten meat in their mouth.
3906. A jawbone of a predatory creature. All its teeth are serrated and anyone holding the jawbone feels as if they are starving.
3907. A jester’s hat made of two different Random Colors and fringed with small bells that jingle constantly
3908. A jigsaw puzzle consisting of occult symbols that when fully completed opens a portal to that which the user desires most in the world. There are three pieces missing.
3909. A joined oak stool with troll-shin legs.
3910. A jointed ceramic finger on a leather thong. A nub of bent and broken metal, whereupon the thong is tied, sticks from the stump like an end of bone ripped from its joint in a whole hand. The finger’s two joints may be manipulated with some difficulty, articulated over some rusty armature of metallic bone.
3911. A joyfully coloured terracotta rattle with a skull motif subtly woven into the pattern of hues.
3912. A judge’s flowing wig of bouncy white ringlets that seems to convey to its bearer an impression of dignity and gravitas.
3913. A keepsake box with trinkets that probably belong to a local commoner. None of them appear valuable in the monetary sense but they undoubtedly hold tremendous sentimental value. Engraved on the lid are the initials S.N.
3914. A key carved from bone that will fit most locks but will break off when turned, jamming the locking mechanism with shards of bone.
3915. A knapped flint arrow head with unknown script upon it.
3916. A knife that belonged to a relative
3917. A knife with a handle made from the fossilized bone of an extinct predator.
3918. A knight’s banner consisting of a vertical black rectangle with a black rook eclipsing a white sun on a field of black and purple.
3919. A knit scarf that releases a soothing odour when you’re scared or hurt
3920. A knotted garment that fades in and out of nothingness. Knowledgeable PC’s know that an order of religious monks one covered their eyes with such bindings. It is a perilous act to stare directly into the mouth of infinity. But once unburdened by vision, salvation shall be revealed.
3921. A knotted gland consisting of a cancerous mass of gnarled tissues. The tissue thumps with an irregular cadence, as if two hearts are intertwined in this tangled clump. The longer it’s held, the more clear if becomes that a multitude is contained within one’s own flesh.
3922. A knotted wooden box that can only be opened by pushing on four specific knots simultaneously. It contains a bright red rod about the size of a forefinger and is decorated with lines which connect in a continuous pattern.
3923. A kraken like statuette, carved from blue and white coral.
3924. A lace-trimmed white silk handkerchief embroidered with the initials ‘FLS’ and stained in one corner with fresh blood.
3925. A lacquered wood-carved mouth instrument resembles a duck-hunter’s call, except for the red band along the mouthpiece and the hooked dragon’s claw wood-burned into its side. Blowing into the object causes it to sound like a drake’s mating call, barely resistible to any draconic creature within earshot.
3926. A lady’s ankle bracelet designed to look like rutting unicorns.
3927. A lady’s coin purse containing two sharp fangs.
3928. A lady’s painted fan, but the painting changes every time it’s opened and closed.
3929. A lantern that cackles eerily when lit.
3930. A lantern with a bejewelled hood. When lowered, it casts multicoloured light.
3931. A large amber stone, encasing a six inch dragonfly with elongated tentacles instead of mandibles.
3932. A large and imposing hand fan, colored deep blue, and decorated with the image of waves.
3933. A large and realistic fake mustache.
3934. A large belt buckle in the startling likeness of a ladybug. When worn, the bearer may occasionally feel a light tickle.
3935. A large block of soap in a waterproof wooden box, which advertises the contents as a lice and flea remover.
3936. A large blue banner decorated with a flaring yellow crest and mounted on the end of a ten foot brass pole. It is meant to be the standard used to keep the morale of the troops high as its valiantly held aloft.
3937. A large blue coin with a powdery finish, perhaps tarnished. A hole is punched in one side, lending it the appearance of a tag. One side bears rows of blocky characters; a lost tongue. The other shows a many-towered skyline, all spires and soaring onion domes.
3938. A large brass medal of military service. It once bore an intricate casting of a lion’s head. But it has been polished smooth over many years.
3939. A large bronze key inscribed with the emblem of a skull and two crossed swords.
3940. A large canvas sack, commonly referred to as a bear bag. It is attached to a 20-foot-long rope, from which it can be easily suspended from a tree limb or similar anchor point. Placing foodstuffs or fragile materials in hanging sacks is often considered a basic precaution when camping in the wild, to prevent bears and other predators from rooting through the sack’s contents.
3941. A large ceramic jar covered in silver leaf.
3942. A large chunk of amber containing a large, green tinted, preserved eye of an unknown animal. The pupil is a small, horizontal slit.
3943. A large conch shell that, when put to the ear, makes the wielder hear the sounds of the ocean. If the bearer closes his eyes while doing so, he will see visions of infinite horizons and calming ocean waves.
3944. A large cowrie shell in the shape of a crescent moon, incorporated into a cloak clasp. Knowledgeable PC’s will know that these shells are sometimes claimed as natural wards against lycanthropes.
3945. A large crystal vase trimmed in silver, with a dozen dead roses and some stagnant water within.
3946. A large decorative candle. When lit, it gives off an alluring scent which, while impossible to identify, evokes a feeling of nostalgia in anyone who smells it.
3947. A large dried, hollowed gourd filled with dried herbs and vegetables. If an amount of boiling water is poured into the gourd, stirred gently and left to sit for a few minutes, the result is a tasty and nutritious vegetable soup.
3948. A large eagle feather that smells of brimstone.
3949. A large envelope filled with papers and documents. It outlines a the events of a murder trial, and how the main suspect is innocent. The name of the town where she is being held has been scratched off of every piece of parchment.
3950. A large forest green tapestry bearing a symbol stretching across its length switched in gilded thread. Its accented with white and red and all along its hem is a complicated mantra of magical sigils and signs.
3951. A large glass jar filled with a gelatinous pink slime. The slime has a humanoid face with eyes and a mouth. It slithers around and eats any food it comes into contact with, smiling whenever it does so. If the slime goes more than 24 hours without being fed it will turn a charcoal grey and will frown until fed again.
3952. A large glass jar filled with pumpkin seeds, one of which has sprouted.
3953. A large glass jug, stoppered tightly. Inside appears to be a diorama of a small forested island with a port town. If left undisturbed for a time, observers may notice that the water surrounding the island seems to move, and the trees wave. At night, tiny flickering lights can be seen in the town.
3954. A large glowing key with an arcane rune of travel etched into the handle.
3955. A large hourglass labelled “The World Entire.” There isn’t much sand left in the top bulb.
3956. A large hourglass which in place of sand, has dozens of tiny teeth of all shapes and kinds flowing between the two bulbs, each one glowing with a faint red light. The flow of the device switches directions at random times for no visible reason, with no bulb ever holding all the teeth.
3957. A large incense burner depicting an apocalyptic scene; A palace and surrounding town is levelled by an earthquake and fire. Dancing tiger-headed demons laugh and dance and whip the dying locals
3958. A large iron key displaying a symbol of a black lion on it’s handle
3959. A large leather tube containing a finely crafted rug of intricate, geometric designs worked with silver threading. Careful examination reveals an inscription in dwarvish runes laced in the threading: “When the sky boils, the sentinel returns.”
3960. A large locket, its case and door crafted from faceted, cracked, yellowed glass cut like a rectangular gem. Its interior frame holds not a painting, nor an etching, but a fuzzy, silver mirror. The mirror, when polished, has a hidden effect: If one looks into it, centers their face in the frame, and focuses upon the background, they may discern a tall, unmoving woman there, towering behind them. She is ghostly, as if cut into the silver, and looks on with deep, piercing eyes. A pair of long, spiraling horns extend from her gaunt and mirthless head.
3961. A large map of the nearby local populace. A number of houses of prominent townsfolk are circled though; several have Xs through the circle.
3962. A large mummified paw of long dead beast. Dried blood stains the fur around the petrified claws which are still razor sharp.
3963. A large musical horn carved from the tusk of a mammoth and decorated with gold bands.
3964. A large obsidian sphere with jutting shapes carved in and sticking out. Each surface is intricately carved with a complex script composed of squares. It is wrapped several times and held inside a smooth pottery sphere.
3965. A large oil painting of some otherworldly sea where creatures who are octopoid from the neck down but with human heads float in bliss.
3966. A large padded envelope containing a single silvered crossbow bolt and a note that reads; “You know what must be done. Make the right choice.”
3967. A large painting of a young boy petting a Cocker Spaniel. Both the boy’s and dog’s eyes are painted black, but somehow still seem to follow whoever walks past it.
3968. A large painting portraying the wonders of underwater life. The viewer see merfolk, sea elves, and all manner of aquatic creatures going about their lives. The edges of the driftwood frame around the canvas is dripping seawater.
3969. A large piece of bleached linen cloth with a stain in the middle in the shape of a demon.
3970. A large poster that reads; “Diplomat wanted. Must fluently speak the oceanic dialect of High Draconic. Come dressed in waterproof clothes to the beach by moonrise on the seventh full moon of the year.”
3971. A large pot scarab filled with mummified human fingers.
3972. A large riding crop with steel studs in it. A creature hit by it immediately develops a series of bloody welts in their skin which spell out the word “Ouch”.
3973. A large sack containing a half dozen pewter goblets along with three empty wineskins
3974. A large saddle with several extra straps and buckles which seems to be made for a winged mount.
3975. A large scroll tube containing a rolled up canvas painting of a sinister looking brooding royal
3976. A large silk flag for a fallen kingdom.
3977. A large sponge that only soaks up blood
3978. A large stone needle carved with figures running from a pyramid with a single eye floating above it; the eye is made from obsidian and set with a ruby centre.
3979. A large suitcase containing a croquet set. It includes four wooden mallets, nine wooden wickets (goals), and four wooden balls.
3980. A large suitcase contains a plethora of items useful to anyone pretending to channel spirits. The case contains candles, mirrors, a ten-foot-long telescoping rod, as well as a selection of specialty items: rigged chalkboards for spirit writing, mundane spirit trumpets for transmitting spirit voices, lengths of gaffed ropes for binding mediums during test seances, and luminous paints and ribbons of fine gauze for fake spectral manifestations. This case obvious belonged to a fraudulent medium.
3981. A large tapestry depicting an ancient battle with tentacled monsters.
3982. A large tapestry made from an unidentifiable thread. Strange symbols and stranger images fill every space, chaotically strewn about the thread work with no apparent pattern. No centralized theme or focus can be made out, but the likeliness of several important figures and deities can be made out amongst alien creatures and other, unknown people. The tapestry is unfinished on one side, making it obvious that the project is still a work in progress.
3983. A large tin canister filled with dozens of mints that when eaten, change the creature’s tongue to a Random Colour for 1d6 hours.
3984. A large tin canister filled with dozens of small red and white striped, mint candies that each have the holy symbol of the God of Trickery stamped on one side and assorted meaningful verses from that deity’s holy text on the other. The canister’s label proclaims in large bold font that the contents are “Testamints” and are appropriate for after masses or meals. It’s unknown whether they are hilarious or blasphemous, which may be the entire point.
3985. A large tin canister filled with dozens of sticks of meat flavored chewing gum. Any creature can clearly identify the flavor as “meat” but as to the exact animal the flavor came from, (If it is only from a single species of animal) it is impossible to tell.
3986. A large tin canister whose lid is stamped with the image of a bountiful orchard whose trees are overflowing with fruit, the ripest of which has fallen and filled a cornucopia. The container is brimming with dozens of pieces of well preserved dried figs.
3987. A large tin canister whose lid is stamped with the image of a bountiful orchard whose trees are overflowing with fruit, the ripest of which has fallen and filled a cornucopia. The container is brimming with dozens of pieces of well preserved dried lemons.
3988. A large tin canister whose lid is stamped with the image of a bountiful orchard whose trees are overflowing with fruit, the ripest of which has fallen and filled a cornucopia. The container is brimming with dozens of pieces of well preserved dried oranges.
3989. A large tin canister whose lid is stamped with the image of a bountiful orchard whose trees are overflowing with fruit, the ripest of which has fallen and filled a cornucopia. The container is brimming with dozens of well preserved raisins.
3990. A large tin canister whose lid is stamped with the image of a bountiful orchard whose trees are overflowing with fruit, the ripest of which has fallen and filled a cornucopia. The container is brimming with dozens of well preserved, dried cranberries.
3991. A large tin canister whose lid is stamped with the image of a lush field, filled with a dozen sheep and a solitary shepherd keeping watch. The container is filled with dozens of well-preserved strips of lamb jerky.
3992. A large tin canister whose lid is stamped with the image of a rushing river and a half dozen salmon jumping out of the water and being caught by fishermen of the bow, spear and net variety. The container is filled with dozens of well-preserved strips of salmon jerky.
3993. A large tin canister whose lid is stamped with the image of a well-stocked confectionery shop bustling with children. The container is filled with dozens of black, licorice flavoured candies.
3994. A large tin canister whose lid is stamped with the image of a well-stocked confectionery shop bustling with children. The container is filled with dozens of blue, wildberry flavoured hard candies.
3995. A large tin canister whose lid is stamped with the image of a well-stocked confectionery shop bustling with children. The container is filled with dozens of green, lime flavoured hard candies.
3996. A large tin canister whose lid is stamped with the image of a well-stocked confectionery shop bustling with children. The container is filled with dozens of orange flavored hard candies.
3997. A large tin canister whose lid is stamped with the image of a well-stocked confectionery shop bustling with children. The container is filled with dozens of purple, grape flavoured hard candies.
3998. A large tin canister whose lid is stamped with the image of a well-stocked confectionery shop bustling with children. The container is filled with dozens of yellow, lemon flavoured hard candies.
3999. A large tin canister whose lid is stamped with the image of a well-stocked frontier general shore bustling with homesteaders, hunters and backwoodsmen. The container is filled with dozens of well-preserved strips of bear jerky.
4000. A large tin canister whose lid is stamped with the image of barn, whose interior reveals a farmer milking a cow. The container is brimming with dozens of sticks of beef flavored chewing gum
4001. A large tin canister whose lid is stamped with the image of barn, whose interior reveals a farmer milking a cow. The container is filled with dozens of well-preserved strips of beef jerky.
4002. A large tin canister whose lid is stamped with the image of the open plains and a herd of buffalo being stalked by hunters and plainsmen. The container is filled with dozens of well-preserved strips of buffalo jerky.
4003. A large tin canister whose lid is stamped with the image of turkey roost and a small child tossing feed from a satchel onto the ground. The container is filled with dozens of well-preserved strips of turkey jerky.
4004. A large tin of well preserved and barely edible fruitcake
4005. A large travel pouch crammed full of preserved cheeses.
4006. A large travel satchel made of glossy crimson leather.
4007. A large vial made of smokey quartz, whose plug is comprised of compressed grass and glue.
4008. A large whale’s tooth that is finely carved, appearing to depict a young woman drowning at sea. Beneath is the name “Celia” and a few badly worn sentences that might explain her fate, but the words are very hard to read.
4009. A large women’s broach in the shape of a lady bug that looks surprisingly lifelike. Occasionally, the bearer gets a ticklish feeling wherever the broach is pinned to them.
4010. A large wooden box of dozens small painted lead figurines depicting knights, wizards, beasts, and dragons.
4011. A large wooden chest with many unique pelts, wrapped one inside the other. In the center a small jade figurine of a humanoid with a fish-like face. It is extremely cold to the touch.
4012. A large wooden coin that resembles a child’s toy, with a small metallic engraving of a dragon’s claw on its face.
4013. A large wrought-iron sign crafted to read, “Beware of the Wyvern” in a bold script.
4014. A large yellow and blue banded egg, roughly a foot long, which rests in a pile of leather scraps. Knowledgeable PC’s will recognize it as a hippogriff’s egg, which if kept warm and safe will hatch in three months.
4015. A large yellow candle that when lit, smells of old books and tobacco smoke. It seems oddly familiar to all who catch a whiff of it.
4016. A large, black, pirate’s hat with silver thread embroidery in the likeness of a skull and crossbones.
4017. A large, boxed chess set whose pieces are dead, costumed pixies sealed within glass.
4018. A large, brightly colored, decorative tin containing a well preserved fruitcake. The sweet bread is studded with dried fruits, nuts and strongly flavored with brandy which adds both to taste and shelf life. The loaf is so dense and nutritious that a single slice can be substituted as a full meal. The sealed tin can be used as 2d4+1 days’ worth of trail rations.
4019. A large, clear quartz embedded with a seemingly random assortment of tiny golden crystals. Knowledgeable PC’s can identify the mineral as pyrite.
4020. A large, cracked, spiral horn of some great beast, bound in silver and caked in blood. When blown, hot winds and swirling sands erupt from the mouth. All who hear the horn’s call are urged to fight with the unrelenting fury of desert storm.
