5132. A simple wind instrument cut from a reed, commonly known as a whistlecane. They are so easy to make, that skilled bards frequently make and give them away to children-to the parents’ delight or regret.
5133. A simple wooden bowl which contains several apples and a pear, all made of colored wax. Under the fruit, in a false bottom of the bowl is a small key.
5134. A simple wooden picture frame holding a sketch of a regal looking bearded elven lady.
5135. A simple, copper belt buckle.
5136. A simply painted, wooden jester’s mask with an unsettling smile carved in. The eyes show the same glee that a child shows as they burn ants with a piece of glass, not fully grasping the value of life. This mask mildly compels to bearer to tell jokes, pull pranks and play tricks, although cruel and twisted ones more often than not.
5137. A single black envelop upon which is a single silver glyph, lightly afire. You do not recognize the language, nor even the alphabet of the burning symbol. Regardless, while touching the mysterious inscription you are instinctively aware of its meaning, as if the words were branded with eldritch fire directly on the grey matter of your soft humanoid brain; “VACATION APPROVED”. You know in your mind exactly what it says. You wish you did not know. If you are able to ken the ancient eldritch script of the elder primordial beings from before recorded time, you are able to understand that the note was written by an unknown number of entities known as “Station Management”.
5138. A single black feather that somehow weighs five pounds and would make an interesting paperweight
5139. A single black pearl with an oddly slimy feel.
5140. A single bloodstained tarot card: The Seven of Swords (signifying betrayal and deception).
5141. A single bottle of Burglum’s Old Quarry red wine.
5142. A single bronze piece with no monarch profiled on it. Its minting date is seven years in the future.
5143. A single caltrop made from bone
5144. A single cheap chrome shuriken.
5145. A single dragonfat candle which burns with a low red light that won’t disrupt darkvision.
5146. A single earring made of a small mouth tusk that when worn, allows the bearer to speak the language of orcs, but only to say: “I don’t actually speak Orcish. I only know that sentence, and this one explaining it.” The bearer is not granted the ability to understand the language and doesn’t comprehend what they just said unless they are already fluent.
5147. A single earring of an ugly style. A thick, short hoop of reddish, soft metal. It smells faintly of wet iron or raw offal when touched and seems to make the fingers sticky. It attracts insects, large ones, that sit within the loop, when worn, and worry and wash their hairy legs, buzzing lowly in the wearer’s ear.
5148. A single glove with three large fingers that puts out any candle sized or smaller flames it touches.
5149. A single gold earring fashioned to represent a man being eaten by a crocodile.
5150. A single gold tooth. A perceptive PC will notice small bloodstains near the root and indents made from pliers near the upper part of the tooth.
5151. A single iron earring that when worn, allows the bearer to speak the language of Dwarves, but only to say: I don’t actually speak Dwarven. I only know that sentence, and this one explaining it.” The bearer is not granted the ability to understand the language and doesn’t comprehend what they just said unless they are already fluent.
5152. A single lacquered oaken earring that when worn, allows the bearer to speak the language of Elves, but only to say: “I don’t actually speak Elven. I only know that sentence, and this one explaining it.” The bearer is not granted the ability to understand the language and doesn’t comprehend what they just said unless they are already fluent.
5153. A single large pine cone that was somehow fit inside a narrow necked clear glass bottle that’s only slightly bigger than itself. Knowledgeable PC’s will correctly assume that the pine cone was first place in water which caused it to close up its plates, lubricated and wedged into the bottle, rinsed and allowed to dry which caused it to open again.
5154. A single leaf from the great tree Yggdrasil. Despite being separated from the world tree it seems perfectly healthy. Legend has it that it will only begin to wilt as a sign that Ragnarok (The end of the world) is imminent.
5155. A single leather glove that is extremely durable and resistant.
5156. A single lens, hand magnifier with a wooden handle, on the grip is inscribed “For those who seek” in golden lettering.
5157. A single oversized earring. At first glance it is a piece of cheap, brass costume jewellery. However, cunningly hidden within is a secret compartment.
5158. A single page from a diary. The writing changes daily, but the material is always mundane and uninteresting
5159. A single piece of parchment on which is inscribed a long list of potion ingredients, their properties, and price in a currency that doesn’t exist anymore.
5160. A single platinum piece that merchants, shopkeepers, bartenders and anyone who operates a business seems dreadfully frightful of and refuses to take it out of superstitious belief.
5161. A single playing card that was stolen from the private game room of the King of Gamblers.
5162. A single raven’s feather that weights five pounds
5163. A single rose, the colour of blood that never seems to wither or require water.
5164. A single sheet of of non-flammable paper that erases itself every night.
5165. A single small pink satin pillow.
5166. A single small, filthy earring that when worn, allows the bearer to speak the language of the goblins, but only to say: “I don’t actually speak Goblin. I only know that sentence, and this one explaining it.” The bearer is not granted the ability to understand the language and doesn’t comprehend what they just said unless they are already fluent.
5167. A single smokeless and odorless candle
5168. A single sparkling earring that whispers optimistic words when its bearer feels sad.
5169. A single steel earring that when worn, allows the bearer to speak the language of the humans, but only to say: “I don’t actually speak Human. I only know that sentence, and this one explaining it.” The bearer is not granted the ability to understand the language and doesn’t comprehend what they just said unless they are already fluent.
5170. A single stick of chalk carved with swirls and twists across the powdery stone.
5171. A single stolen earring that when worn, allows the bearer to speak the language of Halflings, but only to say: “I don’t actually speak Halfling. I only know that sentence, and this one explaining it.” The bearer is not granted the ability to understand the language and doesn’t comprehend what they just said unless they are already fluent.
5172. A single tarnished silver horseshoe.
5173. A single thick metal cuff that was once part of a set of manacles, but has been broken off from its chains and other half. Prayers to the God of Freedom have been violently carved into the steel in several languages repeating a single refrain over and over: “May the Liberator break our chains.”
5174. A single whirring clockwork earring that when worn, allows the bearer to speak the language of Gnomes, but only to say: I don’t actually speak Gnome. I only know that sentence, and this one explaining it.” The bearer is not granted the ability to understand the language and doesn’t comprehend what they just said unless they are already fluent.
5175. A single, blood red, rose that never seems to wilt or require water.
5176. A single, crystalline eye from a giant glass spider
5177. A single, thumb sized, intricately carved gemstone, that upon professional inspection, is completely fake.
5178. A sinister looking lute whose head ends in a wicked spike. The cords are thicker than average and made from thin, woven steel wires. The neck is much longer than normal, and mimics the appearance of a spine. The base is unlike the normal rounded body of a lute, and is pointed and made of a mildly glowing purple rock. The top half of the body is much larger and than bottom, and ends much more jaggedly. The instrument seems to hum with electric power when wielded and any songs played on this lute sound distorted and powerful.
5179. A six chambered spice box containing different varieties of pepper
5180. A six foot long, multi-coloured wool scarf
5181. A six inch bronze fish hook encased in a clear glass cube. Knowledgeable PC’s will recognize this as the third place trophy of the annual fishing competition of a local port city.
5182. A six inch coffin, hand carved from elm. The inside is padded and covered in light pink silk.
5183. A six inch piece of twine dyed a forest green. When in possession of it, the bearer has a knack for doing wilderness camouflage face paint.
5184. A six pieced, wood knot block puzzle. The wooden blocks fit together to form a single solid object and must be moved in a specific way in order to be taken apart.
5185. A six-inch wand of glass that can be bent and even tied in a knot
5186. A six-sided die that sometimes rolls a seven
5187. A skeleton doll, articulated. It’s blackened ivory bones, rather detailed, are joined by small iron rings. One arm is missing, and one eye socket is filled with a small garnet. If carefully inspected, it becomes evident there are two more rows of teeth than normal in the jaw.
5188. A sketch depicting a wilting rose that causes anyone who looks at it to feel bleak.
5189. A skull carved out of charcoal. When burned, the skull will turn into a pile of ash. Within an hour, the skull reforms into charcoal.
5190. A skull covered in tight dried skin, still bearing three gold teeth
5191. A skull of a small rodent with a name carved into it in tiny runes.
5192. A skull of an unknown creature that is covered in ornate tattoos, one of which is the face of someone you used to know
5193. A slab of petrified wood that glows with a soft green hue
5194. A slate tablet on which is carved a prophecy by a famed oracle.
5195. A slate tablet with an exquisite picture of a prominent nearby landmark on it. The chalk image is unfinished and a small box of materials rests nearby.
5196. A slate-grey box covered in tiny blinking lights. Along one edge is a panel which folds down to reveal a seemingly random assortment of letters and numbers on individual buttons opposite smooth black glass.
5197. A sleek corset which includes a patterned brocade of skulls.
5198. A slender belt pouch specially treated to be waterproof. Inside the pouch is a variety of dried herbs. Each bunch is tied together with twine.
5199. A slender chain supporting a small wooden holy symbol of an evil cult’s God. A cultist’s name is etched on the back of the symbol.
5200. A slender hand harp, graceful of design, small and light enough to be played in one’s lap. It is carved of teak wood engraved with designs of waves and fog, with silver wire for strings.
5201. A slender sapphire shard containing a flickering shadow.
5202. A slender scroll tube cleverly carved from an immense fang that holds a single sheaf of tightly wound parchment. The parchment holds a map showing the surrounding area in a simplistic form. The map marks several nearby dangerous locales.
5203. A slender silverwood wand covered with many lines of winding runes of ancient script.
5204. A slick, polished cane with a ferocious sea monster’s head modelled on the top. A hidden trigger causes the monster’s mouth to open and close.
5205. A slide whistle made of bone, carved in the shape of an emaciated skeletal figure, mouth agape at the end. When played, it emits an eerie ghostly sounds that can be varied in pitch with the slide.
5206. A slim wand that leaves a trail of faint sparkles behind as it moves through the air when being used as a focus for casting magical spells.
5207. A slim, blown-glass bottle filled with a shimmering golden liquid. The bottle is sealed with a cork and wax, and the label is hand-written in a language you do not recognize. The fraction 1/500 is neatly penned in the bottom right corner.
5208. A slim, golden hairpin in the likeness of a shrike.
5209. A slip of tattered paper covered in something resembling letters. It seems to be a poem written in an old language.
5210. A slit drum made from a hollowed, fire hardened, hardwood log. The instrument has two slits on its topside, cut into the shape of an “H”. The resultant strips or tongues are then struck with a pair of mallets fashioned from deer antlers which are stored with the hollowed frame. Since the tongues are of different lengths and carved into different thicknesses, the drum produces two different pitches, near a fourth apart. The exterior is decorated with relief carvings of various deities and abstract monstrous designs. Some of these creatures are open-mouthed, providing increased volume through the hole at the end. The drum is one foot long and can be easily carried and played straps about the shoulders.
5211. A sloth’s tooth on which is etched the image of a halfling village
5212. A slowly beating clockwork heart
5213. A slumber, small, shaped like a pint-sized ampoule of green glass. A faint, flickering heartbeat can be heard within, if listened to in absolute silence. The glass emits faint heat, like a living thing.
5214. A small and lightweight wooden whistle. Lazily carved, the instrument features a single finger hole to alter its pitch. A childlike carving of a bird has been hewn heavy-handedly into the whistle’s foot. When putting the reed to your lips and forcing a small puff of air through it, a tuneless squawk sets everyone on edge.
5215. A small and rather ordinary-looking flute carved from a piece of gray driftwood that plays beautiful, clear music.
5216. A small bag containing a set of a dozen 2x2 cm steel cubes.
5217. A small bag containing the complete skeleton of a snake
5218. A small bag filled with assorted types of many-sided dice. Most of them are weighted to bring up high rolls frequently.
5219. A small bag made of chainmail, tightly tied closed and locked. Found inside is a heart made of glass.
5220. A small ball of white marble roughly three inches in diameter. The stone hovers in the air until it is touched or disturbed, at which point it falls to the ground and ceases to exhibit any magical properties until a creature apologizes to it.
5221. A small belt purse containing three copper coins dated seven years in the future with faces of unrecognizable monarchs.
5222. A small black branding iron with a stylized “S” forms the iron’s head. The tips of the “S” end in crude snake heads. The iron is wrapped in bloody, scorched cloth.
5223. A small black metal box that fits under the arm. It has 20 colored pieces of glass arranged in a spiral pattern on one side. With the switch of a lever and the twist of a few knobs on the back , the glass pulse with glowing light at different rates, immediately drawing the eye to their pattern. An noncombatant viewer can lulled into a slight state of relaxation and well-being, being momentarily distracted by the pattern. A bearer can use this as a relaxation tool or as a hypnosis aid.
5224. A small black pouch containing a set of cosmetic tools for cleaning the ears.
5225. A small black pouch of toiletries containing a straight razor, a whetstone, a small mirror, a brush, a cup, and enough shaving powder to last about 50 shaves.
5226. A small black stone statuette of a grinning devil.
5227. A small black stone, when placed in snow it gathers enough snow to it to form a fist-sized snowball.
5228. A small blown glass bird that flaps its wings in sunlight
5229. A small blue disk that seems magnetically attracted to skin and is hard to pull off when its comes in contact with flesh.
5230. A small blue pouch of toiletries containing a comb, scissors, a nail file, a sponge, a hairbrush, a small mirror, soap, a chewing stick, and tooth powder. Essentially all of the tools necessary for basic grooming.
5231. A small blue stone that stays wherever you put it, even if you release it in the air, but it can hold no weight
5232. A small bone whistle carved with symbols and imagery of death. When blown it creates shrill, eerie notes that echos into the distance.
5233. A small bottle filled with dark sand from the Black Desert
5234. A small bottle labelled “otyugh perfume” that really stinks.
5235. A small bottle of eye drops that make the user’s eyes seem completely white with no pupils. This effect lasts for an hour and the user suffer no loss of sight
5236. A small bottle of eyes-burning-from-the-smell-alone wretchedly spicy but delicious hot sauce (Which will cause vomiting and incapacitate the non-spice tolerant).
5237. A small bowl made of bronze. If any liquid but water is put in it, it will take on a salty taste.
5238. A small box filled with different-sized buttons
5239. A small bracelet made up of a series of interlocking clockwork mechanisms and ring puzzles.
5240. A small brass bell that rings a different note every time
5241. A small brass bell whose ringing is only audible to feline creature.
5242. A small brass candlestick that will only allow the candle to stay lit for an hour at a time, no matter the size of the candle. Knowledgeable PC’s will realize that this was likely meant to be used in a bedroom as a precaution against the user falling asleep with candles still burning, thus wasting the wick or risking a house-fire.
5243. A small bronze box containing a tiny wooden owl
5244. A small bronze lamp that was reputed to have once contained a genie before it was freed by a foolish sultan.
5245. A small brooch made of a translucent stone carved into a lily with too many petals. It seems to resonate with power when its bearer casts a spell.
5246. A small brown leather bag contains a double handful of black shale shards that ring like coins as it moves about.
5247. A small burlap bag containing a handwritten love letter, a small jar of strawberry jam and a smattering of pressed flowers.
5248. A small cane box with the words “Eat Me” imprinted on small nickel plate set into the top. Inside is a small iced cake.
5249. A small card marked “Get out of jail FREE” in Common. The local King’s official stamp or good forgery thereof is displayed prominently on back.
5250. A small carnelian carving of a hawk that gives off a faint glow whenever a gnome is within one hundred feet.
5251. A small case containing a simple slate with a grid carved into its surface and a number of differently colored pieces of chalk.
5252. A small case containing metal polish, a small file, a leather paring knife, conditioning oil for leather, two soft cloths, extra leather straps, a sewing needle, and a few buttons. Essentially all of the tools necessary for basic maintenance of an adventure’s gear and equipment.
5253. A small case of big noxious cigars. It’s labeled “Orc Tobacco” but an examination of the contents finds that the “leaves” are a borderline toxic fungus. Smoking the cigars isn’t lethal but the user may wish they were dead.
5254. A small ceramic cat with a perpetually waving arm
5255. A small ceramic pot that produces a tiny puff of colored smoke once every hour and a half
5256. A small charm made out of seashells strung onto a piece of leather thonging.
5257. A small cherrywood box containing 100 needle pins and an instruction manual in the art of acupuncture medicine.
5258. A small chess set whose board and pieces are made of black and white glass.
5259. A small clay bust of a bald woman with instructions to dampen the head daily. If the instructions are followed, after 1d4 days the head begins to sprout long fine vines of poison ivy
5260. A small clay toy pig.
5261. A small cloth doll skewered with needles
5262. A small coin pouch containing five large golden triangular coins stamped with symbols of the minor god of greed, lies and outer beauty. Perceptive PC’s will notice that they are actually lead coins covered in a thin layer of gold leaf.
5263. A small coin pouch filled with ashes. Those who touch the ashes think of having children, or if they already have children, feel a dread that their children are soon fated to die.
5264. A small coin pouch that holds twice as much as it appears it should
5265. A small coin purse containing a set of six dice made from beholder bone, with small etched eye symbols instead of pips
5266. A small coin purse containing a set of six dice made from dragon bone, with small etched dragon skull symbols instead of pips
5267. A small coin purse containing a set of six dice made from unicorn bone with small etched horn symbols instead of pips
5268. A small coin purse containing a set of ten false fingernails painted with mysterious symbols.
5269. A small compass that never points north, and sometimes points at strangers.
5270. A small crystal goblet which makes an unusual ringing sound when tapped.
5271. A small crystal snowglobe that, when shaken, seems to form silhouettes of dancing forms.
5272. A small crystal which, when peered through, appears to show alternate universes. Actually a fragment of a much larger crystal, part of a complex device deep in the Old City.
5273. A small cut glass bottle has a hinged silver top emblazoned with a caduceus.
5274. A small dark rock (Two inches in diameter) that was naturally formed into the shape of a demonic creature with bat wings folded around its body. It is semi-translucent with a dark center that sometimes seems to move. Occasionally it seems to whisper. If one listens closely, the whisper can be understood, but it is a foul and evil entreaty to do some horrible act. A bearer who chooses to obey these instructions, never feel remorse or guilt during or after carrying out the horribly despicable and particularly sadistic actions. Should a bearer who performs evil deeds at the stone’s bequest lose the rock or stops carrying it on his person, the awareness, guilt and shame of his evil deeds comes back to him all at once and he is thrown into deep despair.
5275. A small dirty wooden figurine, that of a crudely-shaped blackbird. Its eyes are glass gems, pupiless; gazing into them feels like falling into an ocean’s black depths. In its tail is a hole, through which one may string a lanyard or band. When you hold it to your ear, you can hear the faint beating of a heart that is not your own.
5276. A small disk that bears the image of the father of whoever is touching it
5277. A small doll that appears to have been pierced numerous times with 4 silver pins
5278. A small dowry chest inlaid with enamel pictures depicting lusty milkmaids.
5279. A small drawstring bag of marbles made from real marble
5280. A small drawstring pouch containing an exquisite, palm sized, crystal clear snowflake that does not melt.
5281. A small drum that when held in one hand, audibly beats in tune with the bearer’s heart.
5282. A small effigy of a small boat with a miniature coffin in the hold.
5283. A small envelope sealed with a wax sigil. If unsealed the letter inside reads: “Meet me at the Red Rose at 7 in the eve. Kill the bearer of this note.”
5284. A small figurine made of soapstone carved into the shape of a snail wearing a saddle.
5285. A small figurine of root and stone in the shape of a large earth elemental.
5286. A small flask made of pale green glass that holds glowing, golden liquid. The flask’s liquid tastes like hot tea, but has an oddly coppery aftertaste.
5287. A small flask of pure spring water. The leather flask itself has a small strap allowing it to be carried over the shoulder.
5288. A small folding chair made of wood and canvas
5289. A small frozen glass-like flame that subtly melts and refreezes, morphing over time. It reflects light (Especially firelight) very well but gives off no heat or light of its own.
5290. A small garden trowel. The blade and handle are made of common, if not poor-quality materials, but the handle is set with a single semiprecious stone.
5291. A small glass bulb that gives off a bit of bluish light when touched. The light it shines counts as moonlight for the purpose of revealing things.
5292. A small glass globe clearly depicting an unknown world. The more a creature looks at it, the more details they discover… as if the globe somehow enhances and concentrates their vision as they examine it.
5293. A small glass jar containing a small, endlessly raining, storm butt. The rain seemingly disappears when it hits the bottom of the jar. A label on the top reads “My Love’s Sadness”.
5294. A small glass jar containing a vibrant Random Brightly Coloured dye that stains the skin for many days.
5295. A small glass jar filled with glass eyes of different colors, one of which moves around of it’s own accord, as if looking for something.
5296. A small glass marble that randomly changes colour every hour.
5297. A small glass sphere the size of a fist is astoundingly heavy, and appears to be mostly full of a thick golden liquid. It weighs ten pounds and has no visible opening or markings on it.
5298. A small glass vial that is always filled with the smell of autumn wind.
5299. A small glider built from the cured wing-skin of a bat stretched over a wooden frame.
5300. A small globe of glass with a city inside, if touched or moved one can hear soft screams.
5301. A small glossy stone on which is drawn a complex runic sigil.
5302. A small gold coin that occasionally twitches. Perceptive PC’s will recognize it as an infant mimic and realize that it will eat other coins it has access to, at a rate of one per day. It will change its appearance to match the most common metal of the coins that it’s placed with.
5303. A small goldfish skeleton preserved in a clear glass orb.
5304. A small grey bottle with a screw-off lid that looks like a piece of common rock
5305. A small half-full wineskin containing a powerful, but harsh, brandy smelling strongly of blackberries.
5306. A small hand fan that only blows hot air.
5307. A small hand mirror that shows someone other than the viewer when gazed upon
5308. A small hand scythe. A strange force burns the hand of whoever holds it that has never harvested wheat by their own hand.
5309. A small handheld box that can project a series of shadows on any flat surface. The shadows seem to be asking for help.
5310. A small handwoven basket with a removable silk lining.
5311. A small harp with a body made out of an opaque golden glass that seem to glow as the instrument is played.
5312. A small harp with little vines for strings and a oaken body with stray leaves. It appears to be alive, requiring water and light to sustain itself. Its leaves change with the seasons and it blooms in the spring. If broken or damaged, it will slowly but surely repair itself.
5313. A small harp with strings that play notes that can only be heard after sunset
5314. A small hood for a trained falcon. Any bird wearing the hood does not need to eat or drink as long as the hood remains on.
5315. A small hourglass holding enough sand to mark the passage of one minute. The bases are made from obsidian flecked with gold and carved to resemble a long-fingered hand holding the hourglass at each end. The glass itself is made from fine crystal and the sand inside is red.
5316. A small hourglass who’s sand only runs when someone nearby is hungry
5317. A small hourglass with a colourful wooden frame and egg shaped glass bulbs. The hourglass contains white sand which takes three minutes to pass from top to bottom when flipped.
5318. A small humanoid figurine carved out of wood that seems to have a slight heartbeat. If held for more than a minute, the beat stops and the creature feels a cold shiver run down their spine. For the blink of an eye, the bearer can see himself from the figurine’s point of view, but then the perspective snaps back and they release the idol.
5319. A small idol depicting a nightmarish creature that gives you unsettling dreams when you sleep near it
5320. A small idol made of bone and crimson gems of unknown nature. It represents the blackened skull of a horned ox with six red eyes that seem to gleam slightly in the dark.
5321. A small imp skull that has been fashioned into a shot glass.
5322. A small inconspicuous black bag containing a collection of dirty implements including: a pliers, a dozen needles, a scalpel, a half dozen clamps of various sizes, a vial of powdered glass, a pair of thumbscrews and a suspiciously clean steel tablespoon.
5323. A small iron sculpture of a phoenix that fills all those who see it with hesitation and unease.
5324. A small iron sphere that always remains one-inch from the tip of a short marble rod, even though they are not physically connected
5325. A small iron statuette of a dwarf, wielding an axe. It’s hard face is set with a murderous expression of cold fury.
5326. A small ivory box decorated with rippling red and green stripes.
5327. A small ivory box, carefully engraved at all sides. It depicts scenes of a nobility: feasts, dances, a marriage, and peasants tending fields. On the inside, it is divided into small square compartments. They were probably meant to store makeup or perfumes, yet none remain. It might have been part of noble bride’s dowry.
5328. A small ivory idol that depicts three tentacles wrapped around each other.
5329. A small ivory statuette of a small human child
5330. A small jar of a sweet smelling green paste. When applied to the tender inflamed skin, it soothes and numbs the pain, replacing it with a pleasant tingling sensation for a few hours.
5331. A small knife forged from a unique metal alloy created by a fallen star.
5332. A small knife, unfit for fighting, with small notches and teeth, like those of a key, cut into its edge. Plainly made from dark, patinated iron. Shiny and sharpened at the hard, toothy edge.
5333. A small knitted octopus
5334. A small lead model of a dragon in flight.
5335. A small leather case containing 12 tattoo needles, and three Randomly Colored inks.
5336. A small leather pouch containing three smoky shards of crystal, some copper wire and a length of braided twine. Each shard is finely etched with an abstract design. When hung and used as a decorative prism in a well lit area, they redirect light into colorful swirling patterns on nearby surrounding surfaces. Adjusting the positions of the shards with the wire allows for changes in the patterns.
5337. A small leather pouch filled with strong-smelling healing herbs.
5338. A small leather pouch of loose smoking tobacco
5339. A small leather pouch tied shut with thin leather cords contains a half-dozen carefully wrapped white mushrooms. The mushrooms are fresh and tasty. They contain a mild hallucinogen that when ingested creates feelings of euphoria and visions of subdued colors bursting forth from any nearby mundane light source. Magical light gives forth more vibrant colors; the more powerful the magic, the more vibrant and scintillating the colors. The effects last for 2d4 hours per mushroom.
5340. A small leather pouch, about the size of a book. Unfastened, it reveals inside several sheets of fine writing paper, a wooden stylus, a wax tablet, two quills and a pen-knife plus a small bottle of ink. A careful examination of one of the sheets of paper will reveal that it bears the impression of what was written on the sheet above it (now long gone). This will be the first half of a letter that gives some tantalizing hints regarding a mystery of the DM’s choosing.
5341. A small living cactus in a crudely decorated ceramic pot.
5342. A small locked box that quietly hums a lovely melody at night but all who hear it cannot recall it by morning
5343. A small looking glass which plays tricks on the eyes. Glancing through it provides normal magnification, but one might see a spire of gold in the shape of a sunlit mountain, or a musical box and floating notes in a butt, or a laughing rabbit in the shapes of the stars.
5344. A small medicine bottle, halfway filled with a herbal remedy.
5345. A small metal bar etched with the word: “Help.”
5346. A small metal box filled with blue powder that, when snuffed, causes profound feelings of ecstasy for about a minute followed by painful cramps that are alleviated only by a messy and noisy bowel movement
5347. A small metal box made of six inward facing mirrors that put off a dim glow which keeps the interior perpetually illuminated. Located inside is a small figurine of a crying angel. The figurine disappears if it is ever outside of the box and is not actively watched by at least one intelligent creature.
5348. A small metal box made of six inward facing mirrors.
5349. A small metal box that makes a clicking noise when in the presence of smoke
5350. A small metal box that produces sounds of hysterical laughter when shaken
5351. A small metal box with broken hinges. When pried open a preserved eyeball will be found inside. If touched the eyeball will share mentally with the user grainy imagery of several hidden treasure hoards but doesn’t provide any clue or direction to the hoards.
5352. A small metal can that contains black coloured pudding. If touched, the pudding leaves a slight stinging sensation to the hand.
5353. A small metal chest with a lock, crafted from iron. It holds one cubic foot, or 25 pounds worth of items. The box is unlocked with its key inside it.
5354. A small metal container that keeps liquid poured inside lukewarm but makes it bitter
5355. A small metal container that makes a loud (but brief) shrill tone when opened
5356. A small metal container that produces a peculiar (but not unpleasant) odor when opened
5357. A small metal cube that, when placed on a flat surface, will emit a cool and humidifying water vapor.
5358. A small metal cube with dark glass on two opposing faces. Holding the box up to a light source causes it to project a colourful image of two men in orange robes, arguing over what appears to be a carefully flayed human skin.
5359. A small metal cylinder with a lens at one end. When looked into, randomly colored geometric patterns can be seen. The patterns change if the end is twisted
5360. A small metal disk with a crystal at its center that causes a buzzing noise in the ears of anyone holding it.
5361. A small metal flask containing a thick, silver fluid.
5362. A small metal pot containing some extremely strong-smelling fermented fish of an unidentifiable type.
5363. A small metal rod that has a tiny sharp wheel on one end and a bulb on the other. Knowledgeable PC’s will recognize this as a glass cutter and know that its function is to score the glass with the wheel and tap it with the bulb to break it. A dexterous or experienced PC will be able to break the scored glass into a single large piece without making much noise. Knowledgeable PC’s are also aware that experienced thieves use glue paper with a glass cutter to reduce noise and capture glass fragments.
5364. A small metal top seems like an everyday child’s toy except for the skull engraved into a button in the middle.
5365. A small metallic rod that glows faintly and makes a whizzing noise when held aloft.
5366. A small metallic whistle with a single blowhole. The pipe is divided into a number of twisted and interlocking tubes, with many holes for the air to exit. This strange configuration produces a cacophonous mess of simultaneous sounds when blown.
5367. A small mirror on the end of a rod that can be used to peer around corners.
5368. A small mirror set in a painted wooden frame
5369. A small mirrored cube
5370. A small model ship that, when placed on water, always points towards the location of the real ship it is linked to currently wrecked off the coast.
5371. A small music box made of brass. It features a pair of tiny automatons that resembles dwarves working at a forge.
5372. A small obsidian horse headed idol with peridot eyes.
5373. A small ocarina-like instrument, carved from glossy yellow stone.
5374. A small orange flag with the symbol of a pair of crossed bone clubs in black on it
5375. A small ornate wood box carved with a serpent dragon design. The box is filled with sheets of thin rice paper in Random Colors that can be easily folded into geometric or animal shapes. The inside of the lid has a few simple instructions for designs and patterns printed on the wood.
5376. A small packet filled with pink dust
5377. A small painting depicting an ugly and extremely overweight troll with a giant club resting on its shoulder, sitting on one side of a cobblestone bridge while a party of adventurers in armor waits on the other side in preparation, their armor gleaming in the sunlight and their spear heads glinting in bloodlust.
5378. A small painting of the Battle of Seymond Run, depicting knights with lances battling frost giants some 300 years ago. The painting is framed with simple wood.
5379. A small painting portraying dozens of stone towers rising up in the middle of the sea. The night’s sky seems to show more miniature towers upside down in the night’s sky with the lights from their tips glowing like stars. On the back of the painting in a corner is written, “There’s always a lighthouse. There’s always a man. There’s always a city” in elegant feminine script.
5380. A small pair of bellows made of wood and treated burlap. Bellows are used for providing steady, direct bursts of air to a forge or a budding campfire.
5381. A small pair of unevenly sized bongos in a travel harness allowing them to be worn and played while moving.
5382. A small pane of stained glass depicting a fire.
5383. A small pearl that always feels warm to the touch locally known as Dragonpearls, used to keep warm during cold evenings.
5384. A small pendant in the shape of a hammer, occasionally harmless sparks fly from it.
5385. A small piece of amber containing a spider in the process of eating a fly
5386. A small piece of apparently unbreakable glass that slowly ripples like the surface of a pond.
5387. A small piece of bleached driftwood encased in a rectangular block of clear glass.
5388. A small piece of bronze hammered into the form of a bearded dwarf’s head
5389. A small piece of dead brain coral. Your mind feels at ease when you hold it.
5390. A small piece of fabric that holds the scent of a lost love.
5391. A small piece of granite in the shape of a rhomboid that occasionally turns to liquid, but always reforms quickly
5392. A small piece of green stone that foams in water and can be used as soap.
5393. A small piece of human leather that has been tattooed with a picture of a six sided die, the number six facing up.
5394. A small piece of natural, unworked stone that, when held up to an ear to it, makes noise that sounds like that of people screaming
5395. A small piece of red and black plaid cloth that always smells of the bearer’s favorite place.
5396. A small pillbox made out of layered purple, metallic scales. A close examination reveals that the scales are metallic, but even a knowledgeable PC cannot identify what creature they originally belonged to.
5397. A small pillow stuffed with soft feathers.
5398. A small pine box with a single word in an unfamiliar language written on top. On the inside of the lid are more foreign words written in a step by step manner. In the box is a tiny, disassembled crib with odd joinings. On close inspection it seems that three pieces are missing.
5399. A small pinkish crystal attached to a piece of leather cord.
5400. A small polished marble disk that resembles a handheld mirror. Any creature who looks into it sees an image of their mother (Or their species’ equivalent) looking back at them, mirroring their every move.
5401. A small polished wooden disk that resembles a handheld mirror. Any creature who looks into it sees an image of their father (Or their species’ equivalent) looking back at them, mirroring their every move.
5402. A small portrait of a family with all of the eyes crossed out
5403. A small portrait of a group of friends, all but one of them with a date written next to their name.
5404. A small pot containing yellow ink, when used it is barely readable but becomes progressively darker as time passes until eventually it is black.
5405. A small pot of rock-salt with Vistani words scratched onto the front of it.
5406. A small pouch containing a few dozen ball bearings that look like miniature glass eyes
5407. A small pouch containing a handful of moss crusted with what looks like dried blood. The blood was in fact taken from a patricide, the moss from a hangman’s tree; the combination makes this quite a valuable reagent to the right buyers.
5408. A small pouch containing common herbs for making tea
5409. A small pouch containing the knucklebones of a deceased human monk who, in life, was renown for her wisdom and skill in combat.
5410. A small pouch containing three quills wrapped in an ink-stained cloth, two small vials of ink (red and black) and several scraps of crumpled parchment.
5411. A small pouch holding three earrings (one bronze pair and a single golden stud) along with a half-dozen rings. All are cheap costume pieces except for the single golden ear stud.
5412. A small pouch made of blue chain mail. When opened on a surface, hundreds of tiny, green ants march out, find nearby small objects (coins, pins, buttons, beads etc.) and begin hauling them back towards the bag. If bag is inspected it is always found to be empty of the ants or anything they have collected.
5413. A small pouch of glass marbles. Each marble has the abstract shape of a different animal embedded in the center of the glass.
5414. A small pouch that has the words “For Panzer only” written on it. Inside the pouch is a sandwich that is absolutely pristine, fresh and mouth-watering. The vast majority of creatures find they are completely unable to remove, eat, or otherwise interact with the sandwich. Only creatures whose given birth name is Panzer can remove the sandwich and eat it.
5415. A small pouch that sometimes has a rock in it, and sometimes doesn’t
5416. A small pouch with a fold-over top holds a half dozen slender knives. All are razor sharp and spotlessly clean. Some have very strangely shaped tips. To a collector of torture equipment (or perhaps an apothecary or necromancer) the set may be worth something.
5417. A small pouch with a moist eye within it. When you bring it into the light, you see the pupil quickly constrict. A PC well versed in religion can tell that this is the living eye of a dedicated follower of the god of orcs. The original owner can still see from this eye.
5418. A small puzzle box that reforms itself after being solved, requiring a new solution in order to unlock it.
5419. A small rectangular device that makes a chirping sound at the same time each day
5420. A small red velvet pouch. The pouch is all but empty, however a determined examination reveals a few flecks of diamond dust stuck to the lining.
5421. A small rock painted to look like a goat’s eye. When in possession of it, the bearer feels uneasy.
5422. A small rock that was once part of an earth elemental
5423. A small rod that emits a voice saying the same thing in an unknown language every time a button is pushed
5424. A small rodent’s heart. It’s still beating.
5425. A small rolled up tapestry, depicting a battle scene between two archmages
5426. A small round coin with a hole in it made of a strange black metal.
5427. A small round grey stone that, when held, gives the user complete control over their pinky toes.
5428. A small round mirror that reflects nothing during the day and reflects only the moon at night
5429. A small round, potted cactus with blossoms that resemble eyes
5430. A small sack containing 30 gold pieces. Perceptive PC’s will notice that they are all fakes, with thin gold plating over lead coins.
5431. A small sack filled with eight gears and springs of incremental sizes that appear to be of the same make or set.
5432. A small sack full of blood-soaked earth destined for the garden of an evil cultist. According to the attached note he believes using blood-soaked earth gives his crops a “certain taste”.
5433. A small sack made of supple, high quality leather, with a dyed design that makes the entire pack look like a chicken when carried
5434. A small sack of shark leather that contains a handful of piranha teeth.
5435. A small sapphire ear cuff carved in the shape of a creature’s fang
5436. A small sapphire hairpin carved into the shape of an ocean wave.
5437. A small scroll case containing scraps of paper that, when ordered properly, become the outline for an epic poem. Based on the outline and the few incomplete verses, this would have been the bard’s masterpiece.
5438. A small scroll tube containing several pages of sheet music for a haunting melody that sticks in the listener’s head if played.
5439. A small sea chest, locked securely. The key is probably somewhere close by. Inside are a dozen glass bottles containing extremely rare and valuable spices. Unfortunately, they are so old as to be unfit for any purpose, and only identifiable by their labels.
5440. A small sea conch with the words “From the beginning” painted on the lip.
5441. A small sealed tin containing two dozen chalky blue tablets. A single tablet that is left to dissolve in water for five minutes will purify up to one gallon, just as if it had been brought to an active boil for ten minutes.
5442. A small set of brass tongs
5443. A small set of pruning shears.
5444. A small sheath designed to be strapped to a forearm and hidden under a long sleeve. The sheath can hold one forearm-length item such as a dagger, dart, wand, or up to two arrows or crossbow bolts. Alternatively, it may store up to one pound of similar shaped materials or small objects. With a flick of the wrist, the bearer can cause some or all of these items to drop into their hand. —Note: Wrist sheaths are typically coloured to match common skin types (Black, green, tan, red scaled, etc.) in order to further hide their presence, assign a colour or pattern as you see fit.
5445. A small shield shaped insignia marking the bearer as a defender of others. This insignia is misshapen as if crushed by a passing wagon and speckled with blood.
5446. A small show globe, akin to the large sort hung in an alchemists’ shops, capped and banded with blackened silver. The screw cap is stuck on but might yield with some twisting. Contains bright blue powder, so fine that a single breath might send it all blowing away.
5447. A small silk pillow has split open here to reveal that it has been stuffed entirely with brilliant golden hair.
5448. A small silver bell that makes a sound like quiet, distant thunder when it’s struck.
5449. A small silver bracelet fashioned in the style of a serpent with two small cyan-colored stones for eyes.
5450. A small silver charm in the shape of a rat.
5451. A small silver dish inscribed with a smiling moon that will fill with fresh milk or food when placed in front of kittens, orphans, and kindly old half-blind men.
5452. A small silver locket that opens to reveal a tiny painting of a severe-looking woman wearing a ball gown. The clasp for the locket’s chain is broken, perhaps it was ripped from its owner’s neck.
5453. A small silver orb with the word “McGuffin” acid etched into its surface. It is coveted by all who look upon it.
5454. A small silver signal whistle whose piercing report can be clearly heard up to a quarter-mile away
5455. A small silver silk drawstring bag containing a set of wings from a fairy.
5456. A small silver spoon. Long, with two slots in its bowl. Any food it contacts develops an acidic flavor; savory, but not pleasant to a modern palette. Any water does so, too, and turns a greasy grey.
5457. A small silver training whistle that emits a high-pitched screech, whose piercing report can be clearly heard up to a quarter-mile away but only by birds
5458. A small silver training whistle that emits a high-pitched screech, whose piercing report can be clearly heard up to a quarter-mile away but only by canines.
5459. A small silver training whistle that emits a high-pitched screech, whose piercing report can be clearly heard up to a quarter-mile away but only by reptiles
5460. A small silver training whistle that emits a high-pitched screech, whose piercing report can be clearly heard up to a quarter-mile away but only by rodents.
5461. A small silver tuning fork. When used, the ringing sound it creates can only be heard by those who have split blood in the last 24 hours.
5462. A small silver whistle which can be easily affixed to a bolt or arrow or spun around in a sling to create a piercing whistle which can be heard for a mile on a clear day.
5463. A small silver whistle.
5464. A small skull carved from lava rock
5465. A small skull fashioned out of an opaque white crystal.
5466. A small sliver of crystal that is completely translucent, although it flickers with a weak glow when held by a living creature.
5467. A small small pane of glass in a frame made of black metal that looks like an ordinary hand mirror at first glance. When a creature spends at least one minute staring into its reflection, the image shifts and changes, showing the landscape of another plane at random.
5468. A small sphere nested within a cube, but the cube exists out of phase and cannot be touched
5469. A small spool of spider silk
5470. A small stained-glass window depicting the crowning of the first king.
5471. A small stamp that creates a bizarre design on anything edible
5472. A small statue of a great old one carved from an unknown type of greenish stone.
5473. A small statue of an mind flayer made of bone, that unnerves intelligent creatures that look it in the eyes.
5474. A small statuette of a man with an eye in the center of his forehead
5475. A small steel box set with a single piece of coral on the top. Each side of the interior is mirrored.
5476. A small stone block from a long-forgotten castle. For some reason, gripping it puts you in a foul mood.
5477. A small stone box containing a single unfamiliar coin of a silvery metal.
5478. A small stone chest containing the preserved heart of a virgin, wrapped in shroud-cloth
5479. A small stone cube that, when tapped with a rod of metal, looks as though it were made of that metal for a one minute
5480. A small stone cylinder that smells faintly of blood.
5481. A small stone face that hovers a few inches in the air, occasionally slamming down to the ground
5482. A small stone figurine carved in the shape of a mammoth.
5483. A small stone hammer, worn down to a nub
5484. A small stone idol depicting a nightmarish creature that appears be half human and half giant worm.
5485. A small stone idol in the shape of a feral, wildcat
5486. A small stone that sings a lullaby in an unknown tongue when you rub your thumb over it
5487. A small stone that ticks evenly like a finely wound clock. Everyone who hears the stone becomes convinced that the stone must remain locked away or something very bad will happen.
5488. A small stuffed vole.
5489. A small sundial on a leather strap that can be worn on the wrist.
5490. A small taxidermied imp, smoking a pipe.
5491. A small tin box with a hinged lid, half-filled with finely ground, pure white, sea salt.
5492. A small tin case containing a dozen facial and ear piercings of varying sizes and shapes, all made from chameleon horn.
5493. A small tin containing a rare, dried, blue flower. When burned, it emits the smell of a salty brine and fresh seaweed.
5494. A small tin full of wrapped caramels, one of which is wrapped delicately in silver leaf.
5495. A small tin of red, sparkling powder, when an undead crosses the powder it glows faintly.
5496. A small travel pouch containing a symbol drawing kit, consisting of a length of string, chalk, compasses and a notebook with detailed notes and diagrams.
5497. A small triangle made of the bones of an unknown fey being.
5498. A small triangular silver coin of unknown origin.
5499. A small turtle shell intricately scrimshawed with aquan script. If translated, it’s a bawdy merfolk limerick.
5500. A small utility knife made of flint with bone inlays
5501. A small velvet bag with a tiny padlocked draw chain. If opened, the bag contains the varnished skull of a human baby.
5502. A small violin that is missing all but one of it’s strings, remarkably when played it sounds as though the other strings are present.
5503. A small waterproof pouch containing four pairs of fur stockings.
5504. A small waxpaper parcel containing the dust from ground up mummies.
5505. A small wheel of cheese that was aged in a cave. The cheese cloth is covered in bat guano, but the label insists that it is good for you and adds to the interesting texture.
5506. A small whistle shaped from ivory, perhaps bone. Yellowed and chipped, thin as a pencil, and perhaps the length of one’s hand. It has eight holes for fingering, but they are of no use, for the whistle produces no sound when played. It vibrates subtly, producing naught but raised hairs and a sensation of anxious, horrid dread in all who are near.
5507. A small wooden bowl engraved with the heads of a snarling lion, bear, a screaming eagle, and a fish’s face.
5508. A small wooden box containing a coarse parchment scroll. It is wound around two thick dowels, and bears densely packed writing, smeared ink, and smells strongly of cedar.
5509. A small wooden box containing a dozen glass fishhooks.
5510. A small wooden box containing a dozen long drinking straws made of bamboo
5511. A small wooden box containing a dozen long drinking straws made of hollowed bone
5512. A small wooden box containing a dozen long drinking straws made of steel.
5513. A small wooden box containing a dozen mismatched silver spoons, all from different sets.
5514. A small wooden box containing a dozen sticks of incense which, when burned, changes in scents. They begin with the rich odor of fine cooked victuals and then slowly sour to the stink of decay.
5515. A small wooden box containing like-new set of six serrated steak knives.
5516. A small wooden box containing ten barbed fishing hooks and a hand carved lure made to look like a small frog.
5517. A small wooden box filled with a dozen polished turquoise buttons.
5518. A small wooden box filled with dozens of black licorice candies.
5519. A small wooden box filled with dried leaves from the Crescent forest
5520. A small wooden box labeled “Ekkio’s Bestiary” that contains a deck of cards which each suit is it’s own monstrous creature type (Such as undead, demons, aberrations and goblinoids) and each numbered card has its own example of a monster of that type with a two sentence description of its powers and weaknesses.
5521. A small wooden box with a picture of a beautiful butterfly painted on the lid. The inside of the box is filled with a cluster of white cocoons.
5522. A small wooden box with a picture of a hideous moth painted on the lid. The inside of the box is filled with a cluster of white cocoons.
5523. A small wooden box with many tiny compartments filled with items useful for feigning a sickness, as well as a reference book outlining common indicators for some of the more feared virulent diseases. The kit includes such things as false pustules, pills that cause the person chewing them to foam at the mouth, and herbal concoctions designed to induce fevers or vomiting.
5524. A small wooden box with some silver markings on its surface. Something can be heard shifting inside, however it has neither a lock nor hinges. Cutting it open by force reveals it to be solid wood.
5525. A small wooden carry case containing five sticks of incense. The box itself is ornately carved and painted but the incense is of poor quality, suggesting the former owner cared more about style and presentation, than substance and mediation.
5526. A small wooden carry case containing five sticks of incense. The box itself is rather plain but the incense is of good quality, suggesting the former owner cared more about substance and mediation, than style and presentation.
5527. A small wooden case containing a dozen steel toothpicks shaped like tiny rapiers
5528. A small wooden case containing a gaming set known as Mazes & Manticores. Inside is a wide variety of items. Maps of made-up continents and cities, sets of polyhedral dice in a variety of colours, quill pens, “Character Sheets”, and a set of large books talking about “XP” and “Levels”.
5529. A small wooden case containing the rules and materials for a game of pickup sticks
5530. A small wooden case containing what is commonly known as a key blank. The two-sided case contains a soft wax that readily takes and keeps an impression of an object pressed between the two halves, quickly creating an excellent mold of the item. A skilled artisan may then make a copy of the item from the mold (Destroying the mold in the process). Although any item can be copied, most adventures will make copies of stolen keys with this case and then quickly return the originals before the owner is the wiser.
5531. A small wooden case holding a collection of pressed butterflies.
5532. A small wooden idol of a cross legged man in prayer
5533. A small wooden sailcart toy
5534. A small wooden statuette of a smug halfling
5535. A small wooden statuette of an unfamiliar halfling-like creature.
5536. A small wooden top. When you spin it you hear the rushing wind, but no one else does
5537. A small wooden travel trunk containing five changes of outfits made for a ventriloquist’s dummy. The outfits are: simple peasant rags, humble priest’s robes, gaudy noble’s clothing, smartly dressed merchant and city guard’s uniform.
5538. A small woolen tapestry depicting a field of flowers with a mountain and stream in the background. The foreground has a trio of men with spades digging up a grass-covered hillock. One of the men has his right arm raised, showing a golden crown to his comrades.
5539. A small, black, triangular stone, about the size of a human’s fist. Engraved in the center is a spiraling mark.
5540. A small, bone, eye mask that protects the wearer’s vision from the effects of heavy snow.
5541. A small, cartouche-shaped plaquette made from bronze, meant to hang around the neck by a chain, like a modern dress gorget. The chain is gone, replaced by simple cord. A sculpted, leonine face looks out from the front, snarling.
5542. A small, clear crystal shaped like a heart
5543. A small, colorful pebble that (No matter what) somehow finds its way back to its owner at sundown.
5544. A small, cork-stoppered crystal bottle with a disproportionately low and fat belly and slender neck containing a ruby colored liquid with an overwhelming odor of citrus. Tasting the liquid brings forth visions of standing atop a pyramid in a desert land, overlooking a sprawling city on a broad river flanked by irrigated farms.
5545. A small, corked, dark glass bottle filled to the brim with a powerful liquor
5546. A small, crystal vial of a clear, viscous perfume that smells strongly of honeysuckle.
5547. A small, decorative pocket knife with a blunt blade made of mother of pearl. Although not functional, it is absolutely gorgeous.
5548. A small, delicately carved hope chest of polished cherry wood. The box contains nothing but a remarkably fine, gray sand taken from some unknown locale, that glows a faint blue beneath the light of a new moon.
5549. A small, elaborately carved silver tube is designed to hold a single piece of chalk.
5550. A small, flat slab of dark stone that, nondescript though it is, gives off an unmistakable eeriness.
5551. A small, framed painting of a castle, the details of which (The number of towers and parapets, the banners flown, siege weaponry on the battlements, and similar features) change subtly when no one is looking.
5552. A small, golden chime, tied with a red ribbon around the handle, that rings softly of its own accord with a bittersweet melody. It makes those who hear it think of sunlight on a coastline that they’ve never seen, holding the hand of someone they’ve never known.
5553. A small, gray cube, carved with intricate patterns that seem to have no end.
5554. A small, handheld harp made from the wishbone of a celestial griffin. It was alchemically treated with elemental fire, laminated with entsap, and enameled with scenes from myth and legend. The instrument is translucent and slightly opalescent in coloration and strung with mithril wire.
5555. A small, heart-shaped token carved out of rose-colored sandstone that contains the mad soul of a princess, trapped there by a sorcerer many centuries ago. The bearer can occasionally make out whispers from caged spirit who claims that her body remains in the hidden royal sepulchres of her homeland, perfectly preserved by ancient magic.
5556. A small, inverted L-shaped machine with a hand crank on one end. The crank seems to power a rather intricate system on the other end consisting of several delicate metal plates and a tiny needle.
5557. A small, iron armature consists of two parallel, studded bars that close together as the frame’s screws are tightened. A knowledgeable PC will recognize this as a thumbscrew, an infamous torture device meant to slowly crush and break fingers and toes.
5558. A small, ivory figure nailed to a Y-shaped crucifix, also ivory. The figure is carved in excellent detail. Though emaciated and wracked in stiff-limbed agony, a wide and tooth grin is present on the figure’s hollow-eyed face.
5559. A small, lightweight, wooden altar made for portability. The top of the altar lifts up, and six white candles, two sticks of incense and a vial of oil are stored within. The altar is modest, but could give a traveling priest a center from which to say his daily prayers, perform minor rituals, or preach to an assembled crowd. Strangely enough the altar doesn’t sport a single holy symbol to any God, perhaps suggesting the previous owner worshiped several at once, or preached an idea of divinity than than the worship of a single divine being.
5560. A small, locked, steel canister which holds a twenty pinches of powdered pearl.
5561. A small, masterfully crafted toy carriage made of stained black walnut complete with leather driver’s seat and interior upholstered in crushed velvet. Anyone who stops to admire the detail of the model in detail has the urge to break one of the wheels, first starting as a fleeting thought but gradually grows to consume their thoughts causing insomnia the longer they resist the urge. The wheel is instantly repaired any time the toy goes unnoticed.
5562. A small, ornate bead that adjusts to fit over any diameter of rope or string
5563. A small, pale blue stone which changes color to resemble a large stone tear when it is hit by the light of the solstice sun.
5564. A small, palm-sized mass of interlocking carvings. The carvings on closer examination resemble five interlocking crescents. The icon is fashioned from what looks to be ancient bone and knowledgeable PC’s can determine that the object was fashioned from the knuckles of five different dragons.
5565. A small, polished, fossilized cross-section of wood. The interior cracks have filled with some opalescent material in shimmering blues and green. Knowledgeable PC’s can identify the mineral as opalized wood.
5566. A small, ragged figure crafted from human bone and hair, posed as though shading its eyes to see a long distance.
5567. A small, Randomly Coloured ceramic jar with a clear glass lid.
5568. A small, Randomly Coloured lizard with brightly scintillating scales that’s been perfectly preserved and entombed in a rectangular block of clear glass.
5569. A small, slightly worn, silver broach in the shape of a dove with an inscription on the back that reads, “To my dearest, Lorael, on the 400th anniversary of your birth.”
5570. A small, soft, black pouch containing a half-dozen lock picks. One is horribly bent and all but useless.
5571. A small, tightly packed suitcase containing two decks of cards, a betting wheel, numbered cloths and four dozen coloured wooden chips.
5572. A small, warm metal blade that gives a very close shave
5573. A small, weightless stone block
5574. A small, well-crafted statuette depicting a hulking metal, box-like figure of a humanoid sitting on a rock in the midst of a creek, holding in its hand a small magenta flower, examining it closely with its eye-less, mouth-less, nose-less, featureless face. The flower is richly colored which starkly contrasts the grey golem. A creature who examines the statue for more than a few seconds feels themselves growing cold and numb and only seeing in shades of grey as if their senses were fading away. The creature’s faculties return to them the moment they stop interacting with the object.
5575. A small, white marble carving of a small spider eating a larger one.
5576. A smallish oaken strongchest, bound in bronze. Inside it rests four fat, tightly-packed pouches of waxed linen filled with a brilliant scarlet dyestuff and three dried bundles of the ruddy, weedy herb of unknown type the dye was rendered from. One bundle is tied with a crude map to the source of the herb.
5577. A smoked glass jar that makes an unsettling noise whenever the top is removed
5578. A smoked glass jar that, when opened, releases a puff of steam that refuses to dissipate until the lid is replaced.
5579. A smoked glass mask bearing a serious expression. Its eye sockets are straight and focused. A nine pointed star is carved into the forehead and the lips of the mask are covered in a thick layer of glossy black lacquer.
5580. A smoked glass urn containing the ashes of a cremated mage
5581. A smokeless and odourless candle.
5582. A smoking pipe carved in the shape of reptilian claw holding an eye, which serves as the pipe’s bowl. The bowl will light itself by telepathic command by a creature that holds it.
5583. A smoking pipe made of glass and metal with a rounded bowl bottom that is meant to be filled with water. The design allows the smoke to be filtered through the water allowing for a smoother smoke while still receiving full flavor of the pipeweed.
5584. A smoking pipe made of multicolored blown glass bent in a semicircle making it look like a rainbow. Smoke blown from the pipe’s contents becomes similarly rainbow colored.
5585. A smoking pipe made with a stem of gnarled wood and a deep bowl made of yellowed bone. The bowl has mystical lettering and runes carved into it.
5586. A smoking pipe used by locals of the area during their war rituals. The tribe’s warriors smoke certain herbs in these special pipes to prepare themselves for battle.
5587. A smoky black precious stone. When held up to the light you can see the back of a devil pressed against the gem, but no matter how you turn it, you can never see its face.
5588. A smooth cylinder made from sandstone
5589. A smooth glass triangle that can store and play back any music the bearer dreamed about.
5590. A smooth green stone that, when palmed, causes strange music to play in the bearer’s mind.
5591. A smooth grey pebble that is always warm to the touch and gives the bearer a feeling of comfort and safety when in their grip.
5592. A smooth grey river stone that briefly glows bright white every third day.
5593. A smooth piece of rounded amber that has a human eyeball in it.
5594. A smooth river stone that when dropped, slowly falls as if it was in low gravity. If thrown it will slow down much faster than a regular throw and gently arc to the ground.
5595. A smooth river stone with shards of bone stuck into it. If you look hard enough you can see that the bones are not of a human but of a goblin.
5596. A smooth, ivory-white cylindrical rod, one foot long and wrist-thick with odd, cursive script carved on one flat end. It feels almost like glass and is always cool to the touch.
5597. A snowball warded such that it cannot melt. At its center is a small glyph-etched stone.
5598. A snuffbox crafted from the dried carapace of a goliath beetle. The box has been sanded, polished, shined and covered in a clear resin so that its naturally rough exterior has been made smooth to the touch.
5599. A soapstone dragon turtle paperweight with a tiny flag on its back that blows in whatever direction the wind is currently blowing in outdoors.
5600. A soapstone jug containing a large wad of ambergris.
5601. A soft cloth handkerchief on which is a limerick, written in blood: There once was a man with one sandal. His appetites most couldn’t handle. He stalks the streets hunting souls. Cooks their feets over coals. And writes limericks about being a cannibal. A soft cloth handkerchief splattered with blood.
5602. A soft cushion that never gets warm
5603. A soft fur stole lined with black satin. Something solid has been sewn within the lining at one end.
5604. A soft leather case containing a vial for holding ink, an ink pen, spare pen nibs, a small container of pigment for making ink, a tiny knife for cutting quills into pens, a blotter, and a small ruler.
5605. A soft, big-brimmed hat that sometimes drips water when it’s not raining
5606. A solid blue, metal sphere, one inch in diameter, with three parallel grooves around the circumference
5607. A solid brass door knocker cast in the form of a winged phallus.
5608. A specially crafted steel cage that looks like it could hold about five rats. It includes stout leather straps around its open end and a metal crucible for holding hot charcoal or other fuel on its top end. Knowledgeable PC’s will recognize this as a torture device used by tightly strapping the open end of the device to a helpless victim’s abdomen, inserting the rats into the cage, and setting the crucible’s fuel alight. The increasing strong heat of the metal crucible causes the rats to gnaw and dig their way through the victim’s abdomen to escape. Panicked rats will chew clear through the victim’s body in order to escape the heat.
5609. A speckled owlbear hide, tooled with raised marks.
5610. A sphere made of steel, which has numerous rods sticking out of it. Twisting the rods of the blacksmith’s puzzle in a particular order allows them to be removed, revealing a gold coin inside.
5611. A spheroid box, small, and ribbed like a squat pumpkin. Splits neatly in two. The halves, which join nearly flush via a thin, inset lip, are conjoined by a short silver chain. Every link in the chain, oblong and blackened, is glassed and opens like a locket. Only one of these frame-links contains a picture: A minute, painted image of a man with a pig’s head.
5612. A spiked red leather dog collar with a steel dog tag on it that reads “Murderface”.
5613. A spindle-shaped piece of blue stone with a golden rod stuck in it that occasionally vibrates and produces fragments of not-quite-human voices, none of which speak a familiar language.
5614. A spindled, skeletal hand of spun glass, jointed with silver wires.
5615. A spool of clear thread that turns to the color you need when you start sewing.
5616. A spool of fine copper wire on a wooden plug.
5617. A spoon made of linen that makes food taste amazing when moving east
5618. A sprig of dried wolfsbane that completely rejuvenates in the light of a full moon and dries out again the following morning.
5619. A sprig of heather that never seems to wilt.
5620. A spyglass lens etched with geometric patterns
5621. A spyglass, dented and bent in half. However because the inside is so reflective, it can still be used.
5622. A square bell, rather small, and unusually heavy. Smooth, unrusted, with a short tongue that wags with only great force. It sheds no sound; only heavy vibrations that shake the hand, vibrate one’s teeth in their sockets, and touch ringing tones of nearby metal objects.
5623. A square digging shovel with the grip and handle stained red with blood. Touching the grip with bare hands allows the bearer to hear the anguished cries of the grave diggers who have died on the job with the shovel in their hands.
5624. A square foot of black cloth that, when unfolded, displays an unknown island with several cities on it. Simple symbols allow panning, zooming, rotation. Buildings with people and creatures are visible living out tiny little lives, but no other interaction is possible. It obvious that this object is under a powerful magical enchantment but it impossible to determine if the magic is some form of divination or scrying and this island actually exists or whether it is a complex highly detailed illusion.
5625. A square foot of black silk.
5626. A square pane of glass set in a bone frame that shows images of animals you’ve never seen.
5627. A squarish iron key, large, with three blocky teeth. One of the teeth rotates, with some difficulty, grinding with rust. The wide, handle portion of the key is also rusted, but depicts, in bas relief, a square door with a howling, heavy-browed face above the keyhole.
5628. A squat hematite idol with blue quartz eyes.
5629. A squat metal cylinder of brass that resembles a small compass. When opened, it reveals a dull blue gem.
5630. A squat stone goblet made of dark green stone with black intrusions, polished to a silky veneer and carved with capering, vomiting frogs and nonsense rhymes in abyssal. Any liquid poured into this goblet instantly becomes a rancid, foul smelling greenish slime.
5631. A squishy ball that randomly changes color when clenched or squeezed.
5632. A staff that more resembles a long and skinny marble column with white pearls embedded along its length. When used as a cane or walking stick, the wielder’s step feels sturdy and secure.
5633. A stained scroll case containing an old chart revealing directions to the Lost Ruin of Sulgaard.
5634. A stained, white leather eye patch with the word “Skipper” stitched on the inside.
5635. A stalagmite tip the size of a fist, that sometimes whispers it’s aspirations to be a stalactite hanging high above all to those who hold it.
5636. A standard copper coin that always lands on its edge.
5637. A standard deck of lacquered playing cards that shuffles itself when tapped twice.
5638. A statuette carved from white wood and splintered at the base. It depicts an aged man leaning forward and cupping an ear, as if listening intently for threats.
5639. A statuette of a six armed man, holding shields in each hand. With a ring-loop for a head, which is looped onto a necklace.
5640. A statuette of a small dwarf-like creature, it’s pose and demeanour seem to change, but only when it is unobserved.
5641. A steel belt buckle that pulses when moving upwards
5642. A steel bowl covered in ornate designs depicting hill giants in combat with dwarves.
5643. A steel bracelet depicting an armored knight protecting a sleeping child from a shadowy monster.
5644. A steel collar set with a coin sized medallion of rare, red flecked obsidian at the front of it. The stone is inscribe with a Necromantic rebuke.
5645. A steel flask filled with water from the Lake of Golden Dreams
5646. A steel flask with a safety latch attached. A sloshing liquid can be heard inside. The flask contains a black, putrid smelling brine.
5647. A steel flask with a safety latch attached. A sloshing liquid can be heard inside. The flask contains a delicious fey honey.
5648. A steel headband set with a glistening cat’s eye gem in it’s center.
5649. A steel hip flask filled with a fiery whisky.
5650. A steel mask belonging to the former king who ruled over the nearby area. That land is no longer is ruled under the same form of government, the mask is a relic from the time the area was ruled by the iron fist of the tyrannical king.
5651. A steel measuring chain. Identifiable as a measuring instrument, as each two-centimeter link is labeled with a careful line and a unit in its end. The units appear to be standard numbers, but with some odd, serif-like additions to each. 110 links, in total.
5652. A steel mirror set in a fanged maw of iron.
5653. A steel plated human skull.
5654. A steel strongbox painted with crude wolf symbols.
5655. A steel thimble that cannot be crushed by any means.
5656. A steel tooth cap, keenly sharp, meant for the upper jaw. Converts the canines and lateral incisors into long, pointed fangs. There are sockets in the fangs fronts, as if they once held gemstones. A pair of blackened, ancient teeth are still stuck within the cap’s inner groove.
5657. A steel-bristle brush, round and palm-sized. The ferule and handle are rusted iron, but the bristles maintain a stainless shine. If the rust is scraped away, the mark of an obscure, two-crested helmet may be seen stamped atop the grip.
5658. A stein decorated with a design depicting a fearsome kraken tearing a ship apart during a tumultuous sea voyage. Golden threads decorate the bottom of the design and line the polished metal lid of the stein.
5659. A stein made of silver and ivory depicting a scene of a picturesque dwarven mountain village.
5660. A stick of blue chalk that fluoresces in moonlight.
5661. A stick of bright green sealing wax that smells like rosemary.
5662. A sticky piece of metal that shows a series of bones and adheres to almost anything
5663. A stone arrowhead. When laid under a person’s head as they sleep it causes pleasant dreams.
5664. A stone burial pillow weighing ten pounds that was originally placed under the head of a mummy. If slept on, the user gains knowledge to the general location of an ancient tomb filled with danger and treasure but suffers from extremely dry cracked skin for the next month. Sleeping on the pillow more than once will cause the user to experience ongoing nightmares about being paralyzed, yet awake during a horrific embalming process.
5665. A stone carving of a furred humanoid creature.
5666. A stone figurine of a snake that changes positions after every full moon.
5667. A stone idol that appears to be a aquatic goblin with a dorsal fin,webbed fingers and a double row of shark teeth.
5668. A stone snail figurine that grows edible mushrooms instead of a shell
5669. A stone statuette of a fearsome basilisk about one-foot in length.
5670. A stone tablet etched with the nickname of a demon and its depiction
5671. A stone tablet inscribed with dark and foreboding runes. When held, the bearer is imparted with fleeting glimpses of legions of undead rising from their graves under the command of the wielder of the tablet’s power.
5672. A stone tablet inscribed with the names of a dozen ancient knights
5673. A stone tablet that bears inscriptions detailing a notable being who fell just short of achieving godhood.
5674. A stone tablet that has been defaced and re-inscribed many times as new religions have overtaken the area.
5675. A stone tablet with a set of religious commandments labeled one to five. The stone lacks any sort or tool or work marks and is absolutely pristine, as though the tablets were created rather than carved
5676. A stone tablet with a set of religious commandments labeled six to ten. The stone lacks any sort or tool or work marks and is absolutely pristine, as though the tablets were created rather than carved
5677. A stone tablet with fine holes drilled through it that seem to be arranged in some sort of pattern.
5678. A stone tablet, broken at one end and covered with odd writing. Each night of the full moon the writing glows and dogs in the nearby area begin to bark.
5679. A stone tablet, with etchings of great heroes covering it. All of their eyes are scratched out.
5680. A stone urn decorated with carvings of vaguely humanoid creatures, that contains ash and chunks of coal. When the lid is removed, faint weeping and cries of anguish echo from within the urn.
5681. A stone with a mouth carved into it, that sometimes has a pinkish tongue
5682. A stoppered clay flask decorated with lewd images of demons cavorting with humans.
